kathaṃ nu khauti saṇṭhānākāraṃ nissāya ‘‘dīghau nu khau ahausiṃ, rassaaudātakaṇhappamāṇikaappamāṇikādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarau’’ti kaṅkhati| kaici pana ‘‘issaranimmānādiṃ nissāya ‘kaina nu khau kāraṇaina ahausi’nti haitutau kaṅkhatī’’ti vadanti|

kiṃ hutvā kiṃ ahausinti jātiādīni nissāya ‘‘khattiyau hutvā nu khau brāhmaṇau ahausiṃ…pai॰… daivau hutvā manussau’’ti attanau paramparaṃ kaṅkhati| sabbatthaiva pana addhānanti kālādhivacanamaitaṃ, tañ‍ca bhummatthai upayaugavacanaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

bhavissāmi nu khau, na nu khauti sassatākārañ‍ca ucchaidākārañ‍ca nissāya anāgatai attanau vij‍jamānatañ‍ca avij‍jamānatañ‍ca kaṅkhati| saisamaittha vuttanayamaiva|

ahaṃ  nu khausmīti attanau atthibhāvaṃ kaṅkhati| yuttaṃ panaitanti? yuttaṃ ayuttanti kā aittha cintā| apicaittha idaṃ vatthumpi udāharanti, cūḷamātāya kira puttau muṇḍau, mahāmātāya puttau amuṇḍau| taṃ suttaṃ muṇḍaisuṃ| sau uṭṭhāya ‘‘ahaṃ nu khau cūḷamātāya puttau’’ti cintaisi| aivaṃ ‘‘ahaṃ nu khausmī’’ti kaṅkhā hauti|

nau nu khausmīti attanau natthibhāvaṃ kaṅkhati| tatrāpi idaṃ vatthu – aikau kira macchai gaṇhantau udakai ciraṭṭhānaina sītibhūtaṃ attanau ūruṃ ‘‘macchau’’ti cintaitvā pahari| aparau susānapassai khaittaṃ rakkhantau bhītau saṅkuṭitau sayi| sau paṭibujjhitvā attanau jaṇṇukāni ‘‘dvai yakkhā’’ti cintaitvā pahari, aivaṃ ‘‘nau nu khausmī’’ti kaṅkhati|

kiṃ nu khausmīti khattiyauva samānau attanau khattiyabhāvaṃ kaṅkhati kaṇṇau viya sūtaputtasañ‍ñī| aisa nayau saisaisu| daivau pana samānau daivabhāvaṃ ajānantau nāma natthi| saupi pana ‘‘ahaṃ rūpī nu khau arūpī nu khau’’tiādinā nayaina kaṅkhati| khattiyādayau kasmā  na jānantīti cai? appac‍cakkhā taisaṃ tattha tattha kulai uppatti| gahaṭṭhāpi ca pātalikādayau pabbajitasañ‍ñinau| pabbajitāpi ‘‘kuppaṃ nu khau mai kamma’’ntiādinā nayaina gahaṭṭhasañ‍ñinau| manussāpi ca aikac‍cai rājānau viya attani daivasañ‍ñinau haunti|

kathaṃ nu khausmīti vuttanayamaiva| kaivalañhaittha abbhantarai jīvau nāma atthīti gahaitvā tassa saṇṭhānākāraṃ nissāya ‘‘dīghau nu khausmi, rassacaturassachaḷaṃsaaṭṭhaṃsasauḷasaṃsādīnaṃ añ‍ñatarappakārau’’ti kaṅkhantau ‘‘kathaṃ nu khausmī’’ti kaṅkhatīti vaiditabbau| sarīrasaṇṭhānaṃ pana pac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajānantau nāma natthi|

kutau āgatau, sau kuhiṃ gāmī bhavissatīti attabhāvassa āgatigatiṭṭhānaṃ kaṅkhati|

vapayantīti viapayanti, ikāralaupaināyaṃ niddaisau| byapayantīti vuttaṃ hauti| taināha ‘‘vapayanti apagacchantī’’ti| apagamanañ‍ca anuppattiniraudhavasaināti āha ‘‘nirujjhantī’’ti|

3. kadā  panassa baudhipakkhiyadhammā catusac‍cadhammā vā pātubhavanti uppaj‍janti pakāsantīti? vipassanāmaggañāṇaisu pavattamānaisu| tattha vipassanāñāṇai tāva vipassanāñāṇasampayuttā satiādayau vipassanāñāṇañ‍ca yathārahaṃ attanau attanau visayaisu tadaṅgappahānavasaina subhasañ‍ñādikai pajahantā kāyānupassanādivasaina visuṃ visuṃ uppaj‍janti| maggakkhaṇai pana tai nibbānamālambitvā samucchaidavasaina paṭipakkhai pajahantā catūsupi ariyasac‍caisu asammauhapaṭivaidhasādhanavasaina sakidaiva uppaj‍janti| aivaṃ tāvaittha baudhipakkhiyadhammānaṃ uppaj‍janaṭṭhaina pātubhāvau vaiditabbau| ariyasac‍cadhammānaṃ pana laukiyānaṃ vipassanākkhaṇai vipassanāya ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina laukuttarānaṃ tadadhimuttatāvasaina maggakkhaṇai niraudhasac‍cassa ārammaṇābhisamayavasaina sabbaisampi kic‍cābhisamayavasaina pākaṭabhāvatau pakāsanaṭṭhaina pātubhāvau vaiditabbau|

iti bhagavā satipi sabbākāraina sabbadhammānaṃ attanau ñāṇassa pākaṭabhāvai paṭic‍casamuppādamukhaina vipassanābhinivaisassa katattā nipuṇagambhīrasududdasatāya pac‍cayākārassa taṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā uppan‍nabalavasaumanassau paṭipakkhasamucchaidavibhāvanaina saddhiṃ attanau tadabhisamayānubhāvadīpakamaivaittha udānaṃ udānaisi|

‘‘kāmā  tai paṭhamā sainā’’tiādinā nayaina vuttappakāraṃ mārasainanti –

‘‘kāmā tai paṭhamā sainā, dutiyā arati vuc‍cati|

tatiyā khuppipāsā tai, catutthī taṇhā pavuc‍cati||

‘‘pañ‍camī thinamiddhaṃ tai, chaṭṭhā bhīrū pavuc‍cati|

sattamī vicikicchā tai, makkhau thambhau ca aṭṭhamā||

‘‘lābhau silaukau sak‍kārau, micchāladdhau ca yau yasau|

yau cattānaṃ samuk‍kaṃsai, parai ca avajānati||

‘‘aisā  namuci tai sainā, kaṇhassābhippahārinī|

na naṃ asūrau jināti, jaitvā ca labhatai sukha’’nti|| (su॰ ni॰ 438-441; mahāni॰ 28) –

iminā nayaina vuttappakāraṃ mārasainaṃ|

tattha (su॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.439-41; mahāni॰ aṭṭha॰ 28) yasmā āditauva agāriyabhūtai sattai vatthukāmaisu kilaisakāmā mauhayanti, tai abhibhuyya anagāriyabhāvaṃ upagatānaṃ pantaisu vā saināsanaisu añ‍ñatarañ‍ñataraisu vā adhikusalaisu dhammaisu arati uppaj‍jati| vuttampi caitaṃ ‘‘pabbajitaina khau, āvusau, abhirati duk‍karā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.331)| tatau tai parapaṭibaddhajīvikattā khuppipāsā bādhati, tāya bādhitānaṃ pariyaisana taṇhā cittaṃ kilamayati, atha naisaṃ kilantacittānaṃ thinamiddhaṃ auk‍kamati, tatau visaisamanadhigacchantānaṃ durabhisambhavaisu arañ‍ñavanapatthaisu saināsanaisu viharataṃ utrāsasañ‍ñitā bhīru jāyati, taisaṃ ussaṅkitaparisaṅkitānaṃ dīgharattaṃ vivaikarasamanassādayamānānaṃ viharataṃ ‘‘na siyā nu khau aisa maggau’’ti paṭipattiyaṃ vicikicchā uppaj‍jati, taṃ vinaudaitvā viharataṃ appamattakaina visaisādhigamaina mānamakkhathambhā jāyanti, taipi vinaudaitvā viharataṃ tatau adhikataraṃ visaisādhigamaṃ nissāya lābhasak‍kārasilaukā uppaj‍janti, lābhādimucchitā dhammapatirūpakāni pakāsaintā micchāyasaṃ adhigantvā tattha ṭhitā jātiādīhi attānaṃ uk‍kaṃsainti paraṃ vambhainti, tasmā kāmādīnaṃ paṭhamasainādibhāvau vaiditabbau|

aivamaitaṃ  dasavidhaṃ sainaṃ uddisitvā yasmā sā kaṇhadhammasaman‍nāgatattā kaṇhassa namucinau upakārāya saṃvattati, tasmā naṃ ‘‘tava sainā’’ti niddisantaina ‘‘aisā namuci tai sainā, kaṇhassābhippahārinī’’ti vuttaṃ| tattha abhippahārinīti samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ ghātanī nippauthanī, antarāyakarīti atthau| na naṃ asūrau jināti, jaitvā ca labhatai sukhanti aivaṃ tava sainaṃ asūrau kāyai ca jīvitai ca sāpaikkhau purisau na jināti, sūrau pana jināti, jaitvā ca maggasukhaṃ phalasukhañ‍ca adhigacchatīti atthau| saupi brāhmaṇauti saupi khīṇāsavabrāhmaṇau|

idāni  ‘‘taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisin‍nau hauti vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī| atha khau bhagavā tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaina tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumaṃ sādhukaṃ manasākāsi| rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ paṭilaumaṃ sādhukaṃ manasākāsi| rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ sādhukaṃ manasākāsī’’ti aivaṃ vuttāya udānapāḷiyā (udā॰ 1) imissā ca khandhakapāḷiyā aviraudhaṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘udānai panā’’tiādi āraddhaṃ| aittha tassa vasaināti tassa pac‍cayākārapajānanassa pac‍cayakkhayādhigamassa ca vasaina| aikaikamaiva kauṭṭhāsanti anulaumapaṭilaumaisu aikaikamaiva kauṭṭhāsaṃ| pāṭipadarattiyā aivaṃ manasākāsīti rattiyā tīsupi yāmaisu anulaumapaṭilaumaṃyaiva manasākāsi| bhagavā kira ṭhapaitvā ratanagharasattāhaṃ saisaisu chasu sattāhaisu antarantarā dhammaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā yaibhuyyaina vimuttisukhapaṭisaṃvaidī vihāsi, ratanagharasattāhai pana abhidhammapavicayavasainaiva vihāsi| tasmā antarantarā dhammapac‍cavaikkhaṇavasaina uppāditamanasikāraisu pāṭipadarattiyā uppāditaṃ manasikāraṃ sandhāya imissaṃ khandhakapāḷiyaṃ aivaṃ vuttanti adhippāyau|

baudhikathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

ajapālakathāvaṇṇanā

4. tassa sattāhassa ac‍cayaināti pal‍laṅkasattāhassa apagamanaina| tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvāti tatau arahattaphalasamāpattisamādhitau yathākālaparicchaidaṃ vuṭṭhahitvā| añ‍ñaipi buddhattakarāti visākhapuṇṇamitau paṭṭhāya rattindivaṃ aivaṃ nic‍casamāhitabhāvahaitubhūtānaṃ buddhaguṇānaṃ upari añ‍ñaipi buddhattasādhakā| ‘‘ayaṃ buddhau’’ti buddhabhāvassa paraisaṃ vibhāvanā dhammā kiṃ nu khau santīti yaujanā| aikac‍cānaṃ daivatānanti yā adhigatamaggā sacchikataniraudhā aikapadaisaina  buddhaguṇai jānanti, tā ṭhapaitvā tadañ‍ñāsaṃ daivatānaṃ| animisaihīti dhammapītivipphāravasaina  pasādavibhāvanic‍caladalatāya nimaisarahitaihi| ratanacaṅkamaiti daivatāhi māpitai ratanamayacaṅkamai| ‘‘ratanabhūtānaṃ sattan‍naṃ pakaraṇānaṃ tattha ca anuttarassa dhammaratanassa sammasanaina taṃ ṭhānaṃ ratanagharacaitiyaṃ nāma jāta’’ntipi vadanti| tainaiva aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ (dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā) ‘‘ratanagharaṃ nāma na ratanamayaṃ gaihaṃ, sattan‍naṃ pana pakaraṇānaṃ sammasitaṭṭhānaṃ ratanagharanti vaiditabba’’nti vuttaṃ|

kasmā panāyaṃ ajapālanigraudhau nāma jātauti āha ‘‘tassa kirā’’tiādi| kaici pana ‘‘yasmā tattha vaidai sajjhāyituṃ asamatthā mahal‍lakabrāhmaṇā pākāraparikkhaipayuttāni nivaisanāni katvā sabbai vasiṃsu, tasmāssa ‘ajapālanigraudhau’ti nāmaṃ jāta’’nti vadanti| tatrāyaṃ vacanatthau – na japantīti ajapā, mantānaṃ anajjhāyakāti atthau| ajapā lanti ādiyanti nivāsaṃ aitthāti ajapālauti| aparai pana vadanti ‘‘yasmā majjhanhikai samayai antau paviṭṭhai ajai attanau chāyāya pālaiti rakkhati, tasmā ‘ajapālau’tissa nāmaṃ ruḷha’’nti| sabbathāpi nāmamaitaṃ tassa rukkhassa|

vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintauti dhammaṃ vicinantauyaiva antarantarā vimuttisukhañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaintau| ‘‘dhammaṃ vicinantau vimuttisukhañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaintau’’ti aivaṃ vā aittha pāṭhau gahaitabbau| udānaṭṭhakathāyampi (udā॰ aṭṭha॰ 4) hi ayamaiva pāṭhau| dhammaṃ vicinantau caittha aivaṃ abhidhammai nayamaggaṃ sammasi paṭhamaṃ dhammasaṅgaṇīpakaraṇaṃ nāma, tatau vibhaṅgappakaraṇaṃ, dhātukathāpakaraṇaṃ, puggalapañ‍ñattippakaraṇaṃ, kathāvatthuṃ nāma, yamakaṃ nāma, tatau mahāpakaraṇaṃ paṭṭhānaṃ nāmāti| tatthassa saṇhasukhumaṭṭhānamhi cittai autiṇṇai pīti uppaj‍ji, pītiyā uppan‍nāya lauhitaṃ pasīdi, lauhitai pasan‍nai chavi pasīdi, chaviyā pasan‍nāya puratthimakāyatau kūṭāgārādippamāṇā rasmiyau uṭṭhahitvā ākāsai pakkhandaṃ chaddantanāgakulaṃ viya pācīnadisāya anantāni cak‍kavāḷāni pakkhandā| pacchimakāyatau uṭṭhahitvā pacchimadisāya, dakkhiṇaṃsakūṭatau uṭṭhahitvā dakkhiṇadisāya, vāmaṃsakūṭatau uṭṭhahitvā uttaradisāya anantāni cak‍kavāḷāni pakkhandā| pādatalaihi  pavāḷaṅkuravaṇṇā rasmiyau nikkhamitvā mahāpathaviṃ vinibbijjha udakaṃ dvidhā bhinditvā vātakkhandhaṃ padālaitvā ajaṭākāsaṃ pakkhandā| sīsatau saṃparivattiyamānaṃ maṇidāmaṃ viya nīlavaṇṇarasmivaṭṭi uṭṭhahitvā cha daivalaukai vinivijjhitvā nava brahmalaukai atik‍kamma ajaṭākāsaṃ pakkhandā| tasmiṃ divasai aparimāṇaisu cak‍kavāḷaisu aparimāṇā sattā sabbai suvaṇṇavaṇṇāva ahaisuṃ| taṃ divasañ‍ca pana bhagavatau sarīrā nikkhantā  yāvaj‍jadivasāpi kira tā rasmiyau anantalaukadhātuyau gacchantiyaiva| na kaivalañ‍ca imasmiṃyaiva sattāhai dhammaṃ vicinantassa sarīratau rasmiyau nikkhamiṃsu, atha khau ratanagharasattāhaipi paṭṭhānaṃ sammasantassa aivamaiva sarīratau rasmiyau nikkhantā aivāti vaiditabbaṃ|

vuttañhaitaṃ aṭṭhasāliniyaṃ (dha॰ sa॰ aṭṭha॰ nidānakathā) –

‘‘imaisu ca aikavīsatiyā divasaisu aikadivasaipi satthu sarīratau rasmiyau na nikkhantā, catutthai pana sattāhai pacchimuttarāya disāya ratanagharai nisīdi| tattha dhammasaṅgaṇiṃ sammasantassapi sarīratau rasmiyau na nikkhantā| vibhaṅgappakaraṇaṃ, dhātukathaṃ, puggalapañ‍ñattiṃ, kathāvatthuppakaraṇaṃ, yamakappakaraṇaṃ sammasantassapi rasmiyau na nikkhantā| yadā pana mahāpakaraṇaṃ auruyha ‘haitupac‍cayau ārammaṇapac‍cayau…pai॰… avigatapac‍cayau’ti sammasanaṃ ārabhi, athassa catuvīsatisamantapaṭṭhānaṃ sammasantassa aikantatau sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ mahāpakaraṇai aukāsaṃ labhi| yathā hi timirapiṅgalamahāmacchau caturāsītiyaujanasahassagambhīrai mahāsamuddaiyaiva aukāsaṃ labhati, aivamaiva sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ aikantatau mahāpakaraṇaiyaiva aukāsaṃ labhi|

‘‘satthu aivaṃ laddhaukāsaina sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina yathāsukhaṃ saṇhasukhumadhammaṃ sammasantassa sarīratau nīlapītalauhitaudātamañ‍jiṭṭhapabhassaravasaina chabbaṇṇarasmiyau nikkhamiṃsu| kaisamassūhi caiva akkhīnañ‍ca nīlaṭṭhānaihi nīlarasmiyau nikkhamiṃsu, yāsaṃ vasaina gaganatalaṃ añ‍janacuṇṇasamaukiṇṇaṃ viya umāpupphanīluppaladalasañchan‍naṃ viya vītipatantamaṇitālavaṇṭaṃ viya sampasāritamaicakapaṭaṃ  viya ca ahausi| chavitau caiva akkhīnañ‍ca pītaṭṭhānaihi pītarasmiyau nikkhamiṃsu, yāsaṃ vasaina disābhāgā suvaṇṇarasanisiñ‍camānā viya suvaṇṇapaṭapasāritā viya kuṅkumacuṇṇakaṇikārapupphasamparikiṇṇā viya ca virauciṃsu| maṃsalauhitaihi caiva akkhīnañ‍ca rattaṭṭhānaihi lauhitarasmiyau nikkhamiṃsu, yāsaṃ vasaina disābhāgā cīnapiṭṭhacuṇṇarañ‍jitā viya supak‍kalākhārasanisiñ‍camānā viya rattakambalaparikkhittā viya jayasumanapāribaddhakabandhujīvakakusumasamparikiṇṇā viya ca virauciṃsu| aṭṭhīhi caiva dantaihi ca akkhīnañ‍ca saitaṭṭhānaihi audātarasmiyau nikkhamiṃsu, yāsaṃ vasaina disābhāgā rajatakuṭaihi āsiñ‍camānakhīradhārāsamparikiṇṇā viya pasāritarajatapaṭavitānā viya vītipatantarajatatālavaṇṭā  viya kundakumudasindhuvārasumanamal‍likādikusumasañchan‍nā viya ca virauciṃsu| mañ‍jiṭṭhapabhassarā pana tamhā tamhā sarīrappadaisā nikkhamiṃsu| iti tā chabbaṇṇarasmiyau nikkhamitvā ghanamahāpathaviṃ gaṇhiṃsu|

‘‘catunahutādhikadviyaujanasatasahassabahalā mahāpathavī niddhantasuvaṇṇapiṇḍi viya ahausi| pathaviṃ bhinditvā haiṭṭhā udakaṃ gaṇhiṃsu| pathavīsandhārakaṃ aṭṭhanahutādhikacatuyaujanasatasahassabahalaṃ udakaṃ suvaṇṇakalasaihi āsiñ‍camānavilīnasuvaṇṇaṃ viya ahausi| udakaṃ vinivijjhitvā vātaṃ aggahaisuṃ| chan‍navutādhikanavayaujanasatasahassabahalau vātau samussitasuvaṇṇakkhandhau viya ahausi| vātaṃ vinivijjhitvā haiṭṭhā ajaṭākāsaṃ pakkhandiṃsu| uparibhāgaina uggantvāpi catumahārājikai gaṇhiṃsu| tai vinivijjhitvā tāvatiṃsai, tatau yāmai, tatau tusitai, tatau nimmānaratī, tatau paranimmitavasavattī, tatau nava brahmalaukai, tatau vaihapphalai, tatau pañ‍ca suddhāvāsai vinivijjhitvā cattārau āruppai gaṇhiṃsu| cattārau ca āruppai vinivijjhitvā ajaṭākāsaṃ pakkhandiṃsu|

‘‘tiriyabhāgaihi  anantā laukadhātuyau pakkhandiṃsu, aittakai ṭhānai candamhi candappabhā natthi, sūriyai sūriyappabhā natthi, tārakarūpaisu tārakarūpappabhā natthi, daivatānaṃ uyyānavimānakapparukkhaisu sarīrai ābharaṇaisūti sabbattha pabhā natthi| tisahassimahāsahassilaukadhātuyā ālaukapharaṇasamatthau mahābrahmāpi sūriyuggamanai khaj‍jaupanakau viya ahausi, candasūriyatārakarūpadaivatuyyānavimānakapparukkhānaṃ paricchaidamattakamaiva pañ‍ñāyittha| aittakaṃ ṭhānaṃ buddharasmīhiyaiva ajjhautthaṭaṃ ahausi| ayañ‍ca naiva buddhānaṃ adhiṭṭhāniddhi, na bhāvanāmayiddhi| saṇhasukhumadhammaṃ pana sammasatau laukanāthassa lauhitaṃ pasīdi, vatthurūpaṃ pasīdi, chavivaṇṇau pasīdi| cittasamuṭṭhānā vaṇṇadhātu samantā asītihatthamattai padaisai nic‍calā aṭṭhāsī’’ti|

aivaṃ nisin‍naiti tamhā samādhimhā vuṭṭhahitvā nisin‍nai| aikau brāhmaṇauti nāmagauttavasaina anabhiñ‍ñātau apākaṭau aikau brāhmaṇau| ‘‘huṃ hu’’nti karauntau vicaratīti sabbaṃ acaukkhajātikaṃ passitvā jigucchantau ‘‘huṃ hu’’nti karauntau vicarati| aitadavaucāti (udā॰ aṭṭha॰ 4) aitaṃ idāni vattabbaṃ ‘‘kittāvatā nu khau’’tiādivacanaṃ avauca| tattha kittāvatāti kittakaina pamāṇaina| nu-ti saṃsayatthai nipātau, khau-ti padapūraṇai| bhau-ti  brāhmaṇānaṃ jātisamudāgataṃ ālapanaṃ| tathā hi vuttaṃ ‘‘bhauvādi nāma sau hauti, sacai hauti sakiñ‍canau’’ti (dha॰ pa॰ 396; su॰ ni॰ 625)| gautamāti bhagavantaṃ gauttaina ālapati| kathaṃ panāyaṃ brāhmaṇau sampati samāgatau bhagavatau gauttaṃ jānātīti? nāyaṃ sampati samāgatau, chabbassāni padhānakaraṇakālai upaṭṭhahantaihi pañ‍cavaggiyaihi saddhiṃ caramānau aparabhāgai taṃ vataṃ chaḍḍaitvā uruvailāyaṃ sainanigamai aikau adutiyau hutvā piṇḍāya caramānaupi taina brāhmaṇaina diṭṭhapubbau caiva sal‍lapitapubbau ca, taina sau pubbai pañ‍cavaggiyaihi gayhamānaṃ bhagavatau gauttaṃ anussarantau ‘‘bhau gautamā’’ti bhagavantaṃ gauttaina ālapati| yatau paṭṭhāya vā bhagavā mahābhinikkhamanaṃ nikkhantau anaumānadītīrai pabbaji, tatau pabhuti ‘‘samaṇau  gautamau’’ti candau viya sūriyau viya pākaṭau pañ‍ñātau hauti, na ca tassa gauttajānanai kāraṇaṃ gavaisitabbaṃ| brāhmaṇakaraṇāti brāhmaṇaṃ karauntīti brāhmaṇakaraṇā, brāhmaṇabhāvakarāti atthau| aittha ca ‘‘kittāvatā’’ti aitaina yaihi dhammaihi brāhmaṇau hauti, taisaṃ dhammānaṃ parimāṇaṃ pucchati| ‘‘katamai’’ti pana iminā taisaṃ sarūpaṃ pucchati|

udānaṃ udānaisīti ‘‘yau brāhmaṇau’’tiādikaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi, na pana tassa brāhmaṇassa dhammaṃ daisaisi| kasmā? dhammadaisanāya abhājanabhāvatau| tathā hi tassa brāhmaṇassa imaṃ gāthaṃ sutvā na sac‍cābhisamayau ahausi| yathā ca imassa, aivaṃ upakassa ājīvakassa buddhaguṇappakāsanaṃ sutvā| dhammacak‍kappavattanatau hi pubbabhāgai bhagavatā bhāsitaṃ paraisaṃ suṇantānampi tapussabhal‍likānaṃ saraṇadānaṃ viya vāsanābhāgiyamaiva jātaṃ, na asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ vā nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ vā| aisā hi dhammatāti| vaidaihi vā antanti aittha antaṃ nāma sabbasaṅkhārapariyausānaṃ nibbānaṃ| imai ussadā natthīti sabbasau imai pahīnattā na santi|

ajapālakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

mucalindakathāvaṇṇanā

5. mucalindamūlaiti (udā॰ aṭṭha॰ 11) aittha mucalindau vuc‍cati nīparukkhau, yau ‘‘niculau’’tipi vuc‍cati, tassa samīpaiti atthau| kaici pana ‘‘mucalauti tassa rukkhassa nāmaṃ, vanajaiṭṭhakatāya pana mucalindauti vutta’’nti vadanti| udapādīti sakalacak‍kavāḷagabbhaṃ pūraintau mahāmaighau udapādi| aivarūpau kira maighau dvīsuyaiva kālaisu vassati cak‍kavattimhi vā uppan‍nai buddhai vā, idha buddhuppādakālai udapādi| paukkharaṇiyā nibbattauti paukkharaṇiyā haiṭṭhā  nāgabhavanaṃ atthi, tattha nibbattau| sakabhavanāti attanau nāgabhavanatau| aivaṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvāti satta vārai attanau sarīrabhaugaihi bhagavatau kāyaṃ parivāraitvā| uparimuddhani mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ  vihac‍cāti bhagavatau muddhappadaisassa upari attanau mahantaṃ phaṇaṃ pasāraitvā| ‘‘phaṇaṃ karitvā’’tipi pāṭhau, sauyaivatthau|

tassa kira nāgarājassa aitadahausi ‘‘bhagavā ca mayhaṃ bhavanasamīpai rukkhamūlai nisin‍nau, ayañ‍ca sattāhavaddalikā vattati, vāsāgāramassa laddhuṃ vaṭṭatī’’ti| sau sattaratanamayaṃ pāsādaṃ nimminituṃ sak‍kauntaupi ‘‘aivaṃ katai kāyasārau gahitau na bhavissati, dasabalassa kāyavaiyyāvac‍caṃ karissāmī’’ti mahantaṃ attabhāvaṃ katvā satthāraṃ sattakkhattuṃ bhaugaihi parikkhipitvā upari phaṇaṃ dhāraisi| ‘‘tassa parikkhaipabbhantaraṃ lauhapāsādai bhaṇḍāgāragabbhappamāṇaṃ ahausī’’ti idha vuttaṃ| majjhimaṭṭhakathāyaṃ (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.284) pana –

‘‘parikkhaipassa antau aukāsau haiṭṭhā lauhapāsādappamāṇau ahausi, ‘icchiticchitaina iriyāpathaina satthā viharissatī’ti nāgarājassa ajjhāsayau ahausi, tasmā aivaṃ mahantaṃ aukāsaṃ parikkhipi, majjhai ratanapal‍laṅkau pañ‍ñattau hauti, upari suvaṇṇatārakavicittaṃ samausaritagandhadāmakusumacailavitānaṃ ahausi, catūsu kauṇaisu gandhatailaina dīpā jalitā, catūsu disāsu vivaritvā candanakaraṇḍakā ṭhapitā’’ti –

vuttaṃ| icchiticchitaina iriyāpathaina viharissatīti ca nāgarājassa ajjhāsayamattamaitaṃ, bhagavā pana yathānisin‍nauva sattāhaṃ vītināmaisi|

kiñ‍cāpi…pai॰… cintaituṃ yuttanti aittha kaici vadanti ‘‘uṇhaggahaṇaṃ bhaugaparikkhaipassa vipulabhāvakaraṇai kāraṇakittanaṃ| khuddakai hi tasmiṃ bhagavantaṃ nāgarājassa sarīrasambhūtā usmā bādhaiyya, vipulabhāvakaraṇaina pana tādisaṃ mā uṇhaṃ bādhayitthā’’ti| saupasaggapadassa atthau upasaggaina vināpi viñ‍ñāyatīti āha ‘‘viddhanti ubbiddha’’nti, sā cassa ubbiddhatā upak‍kilaisavigamaina dūrabhāvaina upaṭṭhānanti āha ‘‘maighavigamaina dūrībhūta’’nti| indanīlamaṇi viya dibbati jautatīti daivau, ākāsau| viditvāti ‘‘idāni vigatavalāhakau ākāsau, natthi bhagavatau sītādiupaddavau’’ti ñatvā| vinivaiṭhaitvāti apanaitvā| attanau  rūpanti attanau nāgarūpaṃ| paṭisaṃharitvāti antaradhāpaitvā| māṇavakavaṇṇanti kumārakarūpaṃ|

aitamatthaṃ  viditvāti ‘‘vivaikasukhapaṭisaṃvaidinau yattha katthaci sukhamaiva hautī’’ti aitaṃ atthaṃ sabbākāraina jānitvā| imaṃ udānanti imaṃ vivaikasukhānubhāvadīpakaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi| sutadhammassāti vissutadhammassa| taināha ‘‘pakāsitadhammassā’’ti| akuppanabhāvauti akuppanasabhāvau|

mucalindakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

rājāyatanakathāvaṇṇanā

6. ausadhaharītakaṃ upanaisīti na kaivalaṃ ausadhaharītakamaiva, dantakaṭṭhampi upanaisi| pac‍cagghaiti aittha purimaṃ atthavikappaṃ kaici na icchanti, tainaiva ācariyadhammapālatthairaina vuttaṃ ‘‘pac‍cagghaiti abhinavai| pac‍caikaṃ mahagghatāya pac‍cagghaiti kaici, taṃ na sundaraṃ| na hi buddhā bhagavantau mahagghaṃ paṭiggaṇhanti paribhuñ‍janti vā’’ti|

rājāyatanakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

brahmayācanakathāvaṇṇanā

7. āciṇṇasamāciṇṇauti ācaritau caiva ācarantaihi ca sammadaiva ācaritauti atthau| aitaina ayaṃ parivitak‍kau sabbabuddhānaṃ paṭhamābhisambaudhiyaṃ uppaj‍jataivāti ayamaittha dhammatāti dassaiti| gambhīraupi dhammau paṭipakkhavidhamanaina supākaṭau bhavaiyya, paṭipakkhavidhamanaṃ pana sammāpaṭipattipaṭibaddhaṃ, sā saddhammasavanādhīnā, taṃ satthari dhammai ca pasādāyattaṃ| sau visaisatau laukai sambhāvanīyassa garukātabbassa abhipatthanāhaitukauti paramparāya sattānaṃ dhammasampaṭipattiyā brahmunau yācanānimittanti taṃ dassaintau ‘‘brahmunā yācitai daisaitukāmatāyā’’tiādimāha|

adhigatauti  paṭividdhau, sayambhūñāṇaina ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’ntiādinā yathābhūtaṃ avabuddhauti atthau| dhammauti catusac‍cadhammau tabbinimuttassa paṭivijjhitabbadhammassa abhāvatau| gambhīrauti mahāsamuddau viya makasatuṇḍasūciyā añ‍ñatra samupacitaparipak‍kañāṇasambhāraihi añ‍ñaisaṃ ñāṇaina alabbhanaiyyapatiṭṭhau| gambhīrattāva duddasau dukkhaina daṭṭhabbau, na sak‍kā sukhaina daṭṭhuṃ| yau  hi alabbhanaiyyapatiṭṭhau, sau augāhituṃ asak‍kuṇaiyyatāya sarūpatau ca visaisatau ca sukhaina passituṃ na sak‍kā, atha khau kicchaina kainaci kadācidaiva daṭṭhabbau| duddasattāva duranubaudhau dukkhaina avabujjhitabbau, na sak‍kā sukhaina avabujjhituṃ| yañhi daṭṭhumaiva na sak‍kā, tassa augāhaitvā anubujjhanai kathā aiva natthi avabaudhassa duk‍karabhāvatau| imasmiṃ ṭhānai ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, katamaṃ nu khau duk‍karataraṃ vā durabhisambhavataraṃ vā’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1115) suttapadaṃ vattabbaṃ|

santauti anupasantasabhāvānaṃ kilaisānaṃ saṅkhārānañ‍ca abhāvatau vūpasantasabbapariḷāhatāya santau nibbutau, santārammaṇatāya vā santau| aittha ca niraudhasac‍caṃ santaṃ ārammaṇanti santārammaṇaṃ, maggasac‍caṃ santaṃ santārammaṇañ‍cāti santārammaṇaṃ| padhānabhāvaṃ nītauti paṇītau| atha vā paṇītauti atittikaraṇaṭṭhaina atappakau sādurasabhaujanaṃ viya| santapaṇītabhāvainaiva caittha asaicanakatāya atappakatā daṭṭhabbā| idañhi dvayaṃ laukuttaramaiva sandhāya vuttaṃ| atak‍kāvacarauti uttamañāṇavisayattā tak‍kaina avacaritabbau augāhitabbau na hauti, ñāṇainaiva avacaritabbau| tatau aiva nipuṇañāṇagaucaratāya saṇhasukhumasabhāvattā ca nipuṇau saṇhau| paṇḍitavaidanīyauti bālānaṃ avisayattā sammāpaṭipadaṃ paṭipan‍naihi paṇḍitaihi aiva vaiditabbau|

al‍līyanti abhiramitabbaṭṭhaina saiviyantīti ālayā, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇāti āha ‘‘sattā pañ‍cakāmaguṇai al‍līyanti, tasmā tai ālayāti vuc‍cantī’’ti| tattha pañ‍cakāmaguṇai al‍līyantīti pañ‍cakāmaguṇai saivantīti atthau| taiti pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| ramantīti ratiṃ vindanti kīḷanti laḷanti| ālīyanti abhiramaṇavasaina saivantīti ālayā, aṭṭhasataṃ taṇhāvicaritāni, taihi ālayaihi ramantīti ālayarāmāti aivampaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| imai hi sattā yathā kāmaguṇai, aivaṃ rāgampi assādainti  abhinandantiyaiva| yathaiva hi susaj‍jitapupphaphalabharitarukkhādisampan‍naṃ uyyānaṃ paviṭṭhau rājā tāya tāya sampattiyā ramati, sammuditau āmauditapamauditau hauti, na uk‍kaṇṭhati, sāyampi nikkhamituṃ na icchati, aivamimaihi kāmālayataṇhālayaihi sattā ramanti, saṃsāravaṭṭai sammauditā anuk‍kaṇṭhitā vasanti| taina naisaṃ bhagavā duvidhampi ālayaṃ uyyānabhūmiṃ viya dassaintau ‘‘ālayarāmā’’tiādimāha| ratāti niratā| suṭṭhu muditāti ativiya muditā anuk‍kaṇṭhanatau|

ṭhānaṃ sandhāyāti ṭhānasaddaṃ sandhāya| atthatau pana ṭhānanti ca paṭic‍casamuppādau aiva adhippaitau| tiṭṭhati aittha phalaṃ tadāyattavuttitāyāti ṭhānaṃ, saṅkhārādīnaṃ pac‍cayabhūtā avij‍jādayau| imaisaṃ saṅkhārādīnaṃ pac‍cayāti idappac‍cayā, avij‍jādayauva| idappac‍cayā aiva idappac‍cayatā yathā daivau aiva daivatā| idappac‍cayānaṃ vā avij‍jādīnaṃ attanau phalaṃ paṭic‍ca pac‍cayabhāvau uppādanasamatthatā idappac‍cayatā| taina samatthapac‍cayalakkhaṇau paṭic‍casamuppādau dassitau hauti| paṭic‍ca samuppaj‍jati phalaṃ aitasmāti paṭic‍casamuppādau| padadvayainapi dhammānaṃ pac‍cayaṭṭhau aiva vibhāvitau| saṅkhārādipac‍cayānañhi avij‍jādīnaṃ aitaṃ adhivacanaṃ idappac‍cayatāpaṭic‍casamuppādauti| sabbasaṅkhārasamathautiādi sabbaṃ atthatau nibbānamaiva| yasmā hi taṃ āgamma paṭic‍ca ariyamaggassa ārammaṇapac‍cayabhāvahaitu sabbasaṅkhāravipphanditāni sammanti vūpasammanti, tasmā ‘‘sabbasaṅkhārasamathau’’ti vuc‍cati| sabbasaṅkhatavisaṃyuttai hi nibbānai saṅkhāravūpasamapariyāyau ñāyāgatauyaivāti| idaṃ panaittha nibbacanaṃ – sabbai saṅkhārā sammanti aitthāti sabbasaṅkhārasamathauti|

yasmā ca taṃ āgamma sabbai upadhayau paṭinissaṭṭhā samucchaidavasaina paric‍cattā haunti, aṭṭhasatappabhaidā sabbāpi taṇhā khīyanti, sabbai kilaisarāgā viraj‍janti, jarāmaraṇādibhaidaṃ sabbaṃ vaṭṭadukkhaṃ nirujjhati, tasmā ‘‘sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau’’ti vuc‍cati, yā panaisā taṇhā taina taina bhavaina bhavantaraṃ bhavanikantibhāvaina vinati saṃsibbati, phalaina vā saddhiṃ kammaṃ vinati saṃsibbatīti katvā vānanti vuc‍cati, tatau nikkhantaṃ vānatauti nibbānaṃ| kilamathauti kāyakilamathau| vihaisāpi kāyavihaisāyaiva, cittai pana ubhayampaitaṃ buddhānaṃ natthi baudhimūlaiyaiva samucchin‍nattā| aittha ca ciranisaj‍jācirabhāsanaihi  piṭṭhiāgilāyanatālugalasausādivasaina kāyakilamathau caiva kāyavihaisā ca vaiditabbā, sā ca khau daisanāya atthaṃ ajānantānañ‍ca appaṭipaj‍jantānañ‍ca vasaina| jānantānaṃ pana paṭipaj‍jantānañ‍ca daisanāya kāyaparissamaupi satthu aparissamauva, taināha bhagavā ‘‘na ca maṃ dhammādhikaraṇaṃ vihaisaitī’’ti| tainaiva vuttaṃ ‘‘yā ajānantānaṃ daisanā nāma, sau mama kilamathau assā’’ti|

apissūti sampiṇḍanatthai nipātau| sau na kaivalaṃ aitadahausi, imāpi gāthā paṭibhaṃsūti dīpaiti| bhagavantanti paṭisaddayaugaina sāmiatthai upayaugavacananti āha ‘‘bhagavatau’’ti| vuddhippattā acchariyā vā anacchariyā| vuddhiatthaupi hi a-kārau hauti yathā ‘‘asaikkhā dhammā’’ti| kappānaṃ cattāri asaṅkhyaiyyāni satasahassañ‍ca sadaivakassa laukassa dhammasaṃvibhāgakaraṇatthamaiva pāramiyau pūraitvā idāni samadhigatadhammaraj‍jassa tattha appaussuk‍katāpattidīpanattā gāthātthassa  anuacchariyatā tassa vuddhippatti ca vaiditabbā| atthadvāraina hi gāthānaṃ anacchariyatā| gaucarā ahaisunti upaṭṭhahaṃsu, upaṭṭhānañ‍ca vitak‍kayitabbatāti āha ‘‘parivitak‍kayitabbabhāvaṃ pāpuṇiṃsū’’ti|

kicchaināti na dukkhappaṭipadāya| buddhānañhi cattāraupi maggā sukhappaṭipadāva haunti| pāramīpūraṇakālai pana sarāgasadausasamauhassaiva satau āgatāgatānaṃ yācakānaṃ alaṅkatappaṭiyattaṃ sīsaṃ kantitvā galalauhitaṃ nīharitvā suañ‍jitāni akkhīni uppāṭaitvā kulavaṃsappadīpaṃ puttaṃ manāpacāriniṃ bhariyanti aivamādīni daintassa añ‍ñāni ca khantivādisadisaisu attabhāvaisu chaij‍jabhaij‍jādīni pāpuṇantassa āgamanīyapaṭipadaṃ sandhāyaitaṃ vuttaṃ| ha-iti vā byattanti aitasmiṃ atthai nipātau| aikaṃsatthaiti kaici| ha byattaṃ aikaṃsaina vā alaṃ nippayaujanaṃ aivaṃ kicchaina adhigataṃ dhammaṃ daisaitunti yaujanā| halanti vā alanti iminā samānatthaṃ padaṃ ‘‘halanti vadāmī’’tiādīsu viya| ‘‘pakāsita’’ntipi paṭhanti, daisitanti atthau| aivaṃ kicchaina adhigatassa dhammassa alaṃ daisitaṃ pariyattaṃ daisitaṃ, kau atthau daisitaināti vuttaṃ hauti| rāgadausaparaitaihīti rāgadausaphuṭṭhaihi, phuṭṭhavisaina viya sappaina rāgaina dausaina ca samphuṭṭhaihi abhibhūtaihīti  atthau| atha vā rāgadausaparaitaihīti rāgadausānugataihi, rāgaina ca dausaina ca anubandhaihīti atthau|

paṭisautagāminti (dī॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 2.65; ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.281; saṃ॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.172) nic‍cagāhādīnaṃ paṭisautaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattā asubhanti aivaṃ gataṃ pavattaṃ catusac‍cadhammanti atthau| rāgarattāti kāmarāgaina bhavarāgaina diṭṭhirāgaina ca rattā| na dakkhantīti anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattā asubhanti iminā sabhāvaina na passissanti, tai apassantai kau sakkhissati anic‍cantiādinā sabhāvaina yāthāvatau dhammaṃ jānāpaitunti adhippāyau| rāgadausaparaitatāpi naisaṃ sammuḷhabhāvainaivāti āha ‘‘tamaukhandhaina āvuṭā’’ti, avij‍jārāsinā ajjhautthaṭāti atthau|

appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ namatīti kasmā panassa aivaṃ cittaṃ nami, nanu aisa ‘‘muttauhaṃ maucaissāmi, tiṇṇauhaṃ tāraissāmi,

kiṃ mai añ‍ñātavaisaina, dhammaṃ sacchikatainidha|

sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pāpuṇitvā, tārayissaṃ sadaivaka’’nti|| (bu॰ vaṃ॰ 2.55) –

patthanaṃ  katvā pāramiyau pūraitvā sabbañ‍ñutaṃ pattauti? sac‍camaiva, tadaiva pac‍cavaikkhaṇānubhāvaina panassa aivaṃ cittaṃ nami| tassa hi sabbañ‍ñutaṃ patvā sattānaṃ kilaisagahanataṃ dhammassa ca gambhīrataṃ pac‍cavaikkhantassa sattānaṃ kilaisagahanatā ca dhammagambhīratā ca sabbākāraina pākaṭā jātā| athassa ‘‘imai sattā kañ‍jiyapuṇṇalābu viya tak‍kabharitacāṭi viya vasātailapītapilautikā viya añ‍janamakkhitahatthau viya ca kilaisabharitā atisaṃkiliṭṭhā rāgarattā dausaduṭṭhā mauhamuḷhā, tai kiṃ nāma paṭivijjhissantī’’ti cintayatau kilaisagahanapac‍cavaikkhaṇānubhāvainapi aivaṃ cittaṃ nami|

‘‘ayaṃ dhammau pathavīsandhārakaudakakkhandhau viya gambhīrau, pabbataina paṭicchādaitvā ṭhapitau sāsapau viya duddasau, satadhā bhin‍nassa vālassa kauṭi viya aṇu| mayā hi imaṃ dhammaṃ paṭivijjhituṃ vāyamantaina adin‍naṃ dānaṃ nāma natthi, arakkhitaṃ sīlaṃ nāma natthi, aparipūritā kāci pāramī nāma natthi, tassa mai nirussāhaṃ viya mārabalaṃ vidhamantassapi pathavī  na kampittha, paṭhamayāmai pubbainivāsaṃ anussarantassapi na kampittha, majjhimayāmai dibbacakkhuṃ visaudhaintassapi na kampittha, pacchimayāmai pana paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ paṭivijjhantassaiva mai dasasahassilaukadhātu kampittha| iti mādisainapi tikkhañāṇaina kicchainaivāyaṃ dhammau paṭividdhau, taṃ laukiyamahājanā kathaṃ paṭivijjhissantī’’ti dhammagambhīratāya pac‍cavaikkhaṇānubhāvainapi aivaṃ cittaṃ namīti vaiditabbaṃ|

apica brahmunā yācitai daisaitukāmatāyapissa aivaṃ cittaṃ nami| jānāti hi bhagavā ‘‘mama appaussuk‍katāya cittai namamānai mahābrahmā dhammadaisanaṃ yācissati, imai ca sattā brahmagarukā, tai ‘satthā kira dhammaṃ na daisaitukāmau ahausi, atha naṃ mahābrahmā yācitvā daisāpaiti, santau vata bhau dhammau paṇītau’ti mañ‍ñamānā sussūsissantī’’ti| idampissa kāraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca appaussuk‍katāya cittaṃ nami, nau dhammadaisanāyāti vaiditabbaṃ|

8. sahampatissāti sau kira kassapassa bhagavatau sāsanai sahakau nāma thairau paṭhamajjhānaṃ nibbattaitvā paṭhamajjhānabhūmiyaṃ kappāyukabrahmā hutvā nibbattau, tatra naṃ sahampati brahmāti sañ‍jānanti| taṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘brahmunau sahampatissā’’ti| nassati vatāti sau kira imaṃ saddaṃ tathā nicchāraiti, yathā dasasahassilaukadhātubrahmānau sutvā sabbai san‍nipatiṃsu| apparajakkhajātikāti pañ‍ñāmayai akkhimhi appaṃ parittaṃ rāgadausamauharajaṃ aitaisaṃ aivaṃsabhāvāti apparajakkhajātikā| appaṃ rāgādirajaṃ yaisaṃ tai sabhāvā apparajakkhajātikāti  aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| assavanatāti ‘‘sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā’’tiādīsu viya karaṇatthai pac‍cattavacanaṃ, assavanatāyāti atthau| bhavissantīti purimabuddhaisu dasapuñ‍ñakiriyavasaina katādhikārā paripākagatapadumāni viya sūriyarasmisamphassaṃ dhammadaisanaṃyaiva ākaṅkhamānā catuppadikagāthāvasānai ariyabhūmiṃ auk‍kamanārahā na aikau, na dvai, anaikasatasahassā dhammassa añ‍ñātārau bhavissantīti dassaiti|

pāturahausīti pātubhavi| samalaihi cintitauti samalaihi pūraṇakassapādīhi chahi satthāraihi cintitau| tai hi puraitaraṃ uppaj‍jitvā sakalajambudīpai kaṇṭakai pattharamānā viya visaṃ siñ‍camānā viya  ca samalaṃ micchādiṭṭhidhammaṃ daisayiṃsu| tai kira buddhakaulāhalānussavaina sañ‍jātakutūhalā laukaṃ vañ‍caitvā kauhañ‍ñai ṭhatvā sabbañ‍ñutaṃ paṭijānantā yaṃ kiñ‍ci adhammaṃyaiva dhammauti dīpaisuṃ| apāpuraitanti vivara aitaṃ| amatassa dvāranti amatassa nibbānassa dvārabhūtaṃ ariyamaggaṃ| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti – aitaṃ kassapassa bhagavatau sāsanantaradhānatau pabhuti pihitaṃ nibbānanagarassa mahādvāraṃ ariyamaggaṃ saddhammadaisanāhatthaina apāpura vivara ugghāṭaihīti| suṇantu dhammaṃ vimalainānubuddhanti imai sattā rāgādimalānaṃ abhāvatau vimalaina sammāsambuddhaina anubuddhaṃ catusac‍cadhammaṃ suṇantu tāva bhagavāti yācati|

sailapabbatau uc‍cau hauti thirau ca, na paṃsupabbatau missakapabbatau vāti āha ‘‘sailai yathā pabbatamuddhaniṭṭhitau’’ti| tassatthau ‘‘sailamayai aikagghanai pabbatamuddhani yathāṭhitauva| na hi tattha ṭhitassa dassanatthaṃ gīvukkhipanapasāraṇādikic‍caṃ atthī’’ti| tathūpamanti tappaṭibhāgaṃ sailapabbatūpamaṃ| dhammamayaṃ pāsādanti laukuttaradhammamāha| sau hi sabbasau pasādāvahau sabbadhammai atik‍kamma abbhuggataṭṭhaina pāsādasadisau ca, pañ‍ñāpariyāyau vā idha dhamma-saddau| pañ‍ñā hi abbhuggataṭṭhaina pāsādauti abhidhammai niddiṭṭhā| tathā cāha –

‘‘pañ‍ñāpāsādamāruyha, asaukau saukiniṃ pajaṃ|

pabbataṭṭhauva bhūmaṭṭhai, dhīrau bālai avaikkhatī’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 28)|

ayaṃ panaittha saṅkhaipatthau – yathā sailapabbatamuddhani yathāṭhitauva cakkhumā purisau samantatau janataṃ passaiyya, tathā tvampi sumaidha sundarapañ‍ña sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina samantacakkhu bhagavā dhammamayaṃ pañ‍ñāmayaṃ pāsādamāruyha sayaṃ apaitasaukau saukāvatiṇṇaṃ jātijarābhibhūtaṃ janataṃ avaikkhassu  upadhāraya upaparikkhāti| ayaṃ panaittha adhippāyau – yathā hi pabbatapādai samantā mahantaṃ khaittaṃ katvā tattha kaidārapāḷīsu kuṭikāyau katvā rattiṃ aggiṃ jālaiyyuṃ, caturaṅgasaman‍nāgatañ‍ca andhakāraṃ assa, atha tassa pabbatassa matthakai ṭhatvā cakkhumatau purisassa bhūmiṃ aulaukayatau naiva khaittaṃ, na kaidārapāḷiyau, na kuṭiyau, na tattha sayitamanussā pañ‍ñāyaiyyuṃ anuj‍jalabhāvatau, kuṭikāsu pana aggijālāmattamaiva  pañ‍ñāyaiyya uj‍jalabhāvatau, aivaṃ dhammapāsādaṃ āruyha sattanikāyaṃ aulaukayatau tathāgatassa yai tai akatakalyāṇā sattā, tai aikavihārai dakkhiṇajāṇupassai nisin‍nāpi buddhacakkhussa āpāthaṃ nāgacchanti ñāṇagginā anuj‍jalabhāvatau anuḷārabhāvatau ca, rattiṃ khittā sarā viya haunti| yai pana katakalyāṇā vainaiyyapuggalā, tai aivassa dūraipi ṭhitā āpāthamāgacchanti paripak‍kañāṇaggitāya samuj‍jalabhāvatau uḷārasantānatāya ca, sau aggi viya himavantapabbatau viya ca| vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘dūrai santau pakāsainti, himavantauva pabbatau|

asantaittha na dissanti, rattiṃ khittā yathā sarā’’ti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 304)|

uṭṭhaihīti bhagavatau dhammadaisanatthaṃ cārikacaraṇaṃ yācantau bhaṇati| uṭṭhaihīti vā dhammadaisanāya appaussuk‍katāsaṅkhātasaṅkaucāpattitau kilāsubhāvatau uṭṭhaha| vīrātiādīsu bhagavā sātisayacatubbidhasammappadhānavīriyavantatāya vīrau, daivaputtamac‍cukilaisābhisaṅkhārānaṃ vijitattā vijitasaṅgāmau, jātikantārāditau vainaiyyasatthaṃ vāhanasamatthatāya nibbānasaṅkhātaṃ khaimappadaisaṃ sampāpanasamatthatāya satthavāhau, kāmacchandaiṇassa abhāvatau aṇaṇauti vaiditabbau| yau hi paraisaṃ iṇaṃ gahaitvā vināsaiti, sau taihi ‘‘iṇaṃ daihī’’ti taj‍jamānaupi pharusaṃ vuc‍camānaupi vambhamānaupi vadhiyamānaupi kiñ‍ci paṭippaharituṃ na sak‍kauti, sabbaṃ titikkhati| titikkhakāraṇañhissa taṃ iṇaṃ hauti, aivamaiva yau yamhi kāmacchandaina raj‍jati, taṇhāgahaṇaina taṃ vatthuṃ gaṇhāti, sau taina pharusaṃ vuc‍camānaupi vambhamānaupi vadhiyamānaupi kiñ‍ci paṭippaharituṃ na sak‍kauti, sabbaṃ titikkhati| titikkhakāraṇañhissa sau kāmacchandau hauti gharasāmikaihi vihaiṭhiyamānānaṃ itthīnaṃ viya| kasmā? iṇasadisattā kāmacchandassa|

9. ajjhaisananti garuṭṭhānīyaṃ payirupāsitvā garutaraṃ payaujanaṃ uddissa abhipatthanā ajjhaisanā, sāpi atthatau yācanā aiva| buddhacakkhunāti indriyaparaupariyattañāṇaina ca āsayānusayañāṇaina ca| imaisañhi dvin‍naṃ ñāṇānaṃ buddhacakkhūti nāmaṃ, sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇassa samantacakkhūti| haiṭṭhimānaṃ  tiṇṇaṃ maggañāṇānaṃ dhammacakkhūti| apparajakkhaitiādīsu yaisaṃ vuttanayainaiva pañ‍ñācakkhumhi rāgādirajaṃ appaṃ, tai apparajakkhā| yaisaṃ taṃ mahantaṃ, tai mahārajakkhā| yaisaṃ saddhādīni indriyāni tikkhāni, tai tikkhindriyā| yaisaṃ tāni mudūni, tai mudindriyā| yaisaṃ taiyaiva saddhādayau ākārā sundarā, tai svākārā| yai kathitakāraṇaṃ sal‍lakkhainti, sukhaina sak‍kā haunti viñ‍ñāpaituṃ, tai suviñ‍ñāpayā| yai paralaukañ‍caiva vaj‍jañ‍ca bhayatau passanti, tai paralaukavaj‍jabhayadassāvinau nāma|

uppalāni aittha santīti uppalinī, gacchaupi jalāsayaupi, idha pana jalāsayau adhippaitau, tasmā uppaliniyanti uppalavanaiti aivamatthau gahaitabbau| itau paraisupi aisaiva nayau| antaunimuggapausīnīti yāni udakassa antau nimuggānaiva hutvā pussanti vaḍḍhanti, tāni antaunimuggapausīni| udakaṃ ac‍cuggamma tiṭṭhantīti udakaṃ atik‍kamitvā tiṭṭhanti| tattha yāni ac‍cuggamma ṭhitāni sūriyarasmisamphassaṃ āgamayamānāni, tāni aj‍ja pupphanakāni| yāni samaudakaṃ ṭhitāni, tāni svai pupphanakāni| yāni udakā anuggatāni antaunimuggapausīni, tāni tatiyadivasai pupphanakāni| udakā pana anuggatāni añ‍ñānipi saraugauppalādīni nāma atthi, yāni naiva pupphissanti macchakacchapabhakkhānaiva bhavissanti, tāni pāḷiṃ nāruḷhāni, āharitvā pana dīpaitabbānīti aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pakāsitāni| yathaiva hi tāni catubbidhāni pupphāni, aivamaiva ugghaṭitañ‍ñū vipañ‍citañ‍ñū naiyyau padaparamauti cattārau puggalā|

tattha yassa puggalassa saha udāhaṭavailāya dhammābhisamayau hauti, ayaṃ ‘‘cattārau satipaṭṭhānā’’tiādinā nayaina saṅkhittaina mātikāya ṭhapiyamānāya daisanānusāraina ñāṇaṃ paisaitvā arahattaṃ gaṇhituṃ samatthau puggalau ugghaṭitañ‍ñūti vuc‍cati| yassa puggalassa saṅkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthai vibhajiyamānai dhammābhisamayau hauti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati puggalau vipañ‍citañ‍ñū| yassa puggalassa uddaisatau paripucchatau yaunisau manasikarautau kalyāṇamittai saivatau bhajatau payirupāsatau anupubbaina dhammābhisamayau hauti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati puggalau naiyyau| yassa puggalassa bahumpi suṇatau bahumpi bhaṇatau bahumpi dhārayatau  bahumpi vācayatau na tāya jātiyā dhammābhisamayau hauti, taina attabhāvaina maggaṃ vā phalaṃ vā antamasau jhānaṃ vā vipassanaṃ vā nibbattaituṃ na sak‍kauti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati puggalau padaparamau| tattha bhagavā uppalavanādisadisaṃ dasasahassilaukadhātuṃ aulaukaintau aj‍ja pupphanakāni viya ugghaṭitañ‍ñū, svai pupphanakāni viya vipañ‍citañ‍ñū, tatiyadivasai pupphanakāni viya naiyyai, macchakacchapabhakkhapupphāni  viya padaparamai ca addasa, passantau ca ‘‘aittakā apparajakkhā, aittakā mahārajakkhā, tatrāpi aittakā ugghaṭitañ‍ñū’’ti aivaṃ sabbākāratauva addasa|

tattha tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ imasmiñ‍ñaiva attabhāvai bhagavatau dhammadaisanā atthaṃ sādhaiti| padaparamānaṃ anāgatatthāya vāsanā hauti| atha bhagavā imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ atthāvahaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ viditvā daisaitukamyataṃ uppādaitvā puna sabbaipi tīsu bhavaisu sattai bhabbābhabbavasaina dvai kauṭṭhāsai akāsi| yai sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘yai tai sattā kammāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā vipākāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā kilaisāvaraṇaina saman‍nāgatā assaddhā acchandikā duppañ‍ñā abhabbā niyāmaṃ auk‍kamituṃ kusalaisu dhammaisu sammattaṃ, imai tai sattā abhabbā| katamai tai sattā bhabbā? yai tai sattā na kammāvaraṇaina…pai॰… imai tai sattā bhabbā’’ti (vibha॰ 826-827)| tattha sabbaipi abhabbapuggalai pahāya bhabbapuggalaiyaiva ñāṇaina pariggahaitvā ‘‘aittakā rāgacaritā, aittakā dausa, mauha, vitak‍ka, saddhā, buddhicaritā’’ti cha kauṭṭhāsai akāsi, aivaṃ katvā dhammaṃ daisaissāmīti cintaisi| aittha ca apparajakkhādibhabbādivasaina āvaj‍jaintassa bhagavatau tai sattā puñ‍japuñ‍jāva hutvā upaṭṭhahanti, na aikaikāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

pac‍cabhāsīti patiabhāsi| apārutāti vivaṭā| amatassa dvārāti ariyamaggau| sau hi amatasaṅkhātassa nibbānassa dvāraṃ, sau mayā vivaritvā ṭhapitau mahākaruṇūpanissayaina sayambhūñāṇaina adhigatattāti dassaiti| ‘‘apārutaṃ taisaṃ amatassa dvāra’’nti kaici paṭhanti| pamuñ‍cantu saddhanti sabbai attanau saddhaṃ muñ‍cantu vissaj‍jaintu pavaidaintu, mayā daisitai dhammai mayi ca attanau saddahanākāraṃ uṭṭhāpaintūti atthau| pacchimapadadvayai ayamatthau – ahañhi attanau paguṇaṃ suppavattitampi imaṃ paṇītaṃ  uttamaṃ dhammaṃ kāyavācākilamathasañ‍ñī hutvā na bhāsiṃ, na bhāsissāmīti cintaisiṃ, idāni pana sabbau janau saddhābhājanaṃ upanaitu, pūraissāmi naisaṃ saṅkappanti| antaradhāyīti satthāraṃ gandhamālādīhi pūjaitvā antarahitau, sakaṭṭhānamaiva gatauti atthau| satthusantikañhi upagatānaṃ daivānaṃ brahmānañ‍ca tassa puratau antaradhānaṃ nāma sakaṭṭhānagamanamaiva|

brahmayācanakathāvaṇṇanā niṭṭhitā|

pañ‍cavaggiyakathāvaṇṇanā

10. aitadahausīti  aitaṃ ahausi, ‘‘kassa nu khau ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiyya’’nti ayaṃ dhammadaisanāpaṭisaṃyuttau vitak‍kau udapādīti atthau| āḷārauti tassa nāmaṃ| dīghapiṅgalau kiraisa| sau hi tuṅgasarīratāya dīghau, piṅgalacakkhutāya piṅgalau, tainassa ‘‘āḷārau’’ti nāmaṃ ahausi| kālāmauti gauttaṃ| paṇḍitauti (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.284) paṇḍic‍caina saman‍nāgatau, samāpattipaṭilābhasaṃsiddhaina adhigamabāhusac‍casaṅkhātaina paṇḍitabhāvaina saman‍nāgatauti atthau| byattauti vaiyyattiyaina saman‍nāgatau, samāpattipaṭilābhapac‍cayaina pārihārikapañ‍ñāsaṅkhātaina byattabhāvaina saman‍nāgatauti atthau| maidhāvīti ṭhānuppattiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau| atha vā maidhāvīti tihaitukapaṭisandhipañ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya taṃtaṃitikattabbatāpañ‍ñāsaṅkhātāya ca maidhāya saman‍nāgatauti aivamaittha atthau daṭṭhabbau| apparajakkhajātikauti samāpattiyā vikkhambhitattā nik‍kilaisajātikau visuddhasattau| ājānissatīti sal‍lakkhaissati paṭivijjhissati|

bhagavataupi khau ñāṇaṃ udapādīti bhagavataupi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ uppaj‍ji| bhagavā kira daivatāya kathitainaiva niṭṭhaṃ agantvā sayampi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina aulaukaintau itau sattamadivasamatthakai kālaṃ katvā ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai nibbattauti addasa| taṃ sandhāyāha ‘‘bhagavataupi khau ñāṇaṃ udapādī’’ti| mahājāniyauti sattadivasabbhantarai pattabbamaggaphalatau  parihīnattā mahatī jāni parihāni assāti mahājāniyau| akkhaṇai nibbattatthā idha dhammadaisanaṭṭhānaṃ āgamanapādāpi natthi, athāhaṃ tattha gacchaiyyaṃ, gantvā daisiyamānaṃ dhammampissa sautuṃ sautapasādaupi natthi, aivaṃ mahājāniyau jātauti dassaiti| kiṃ pana bhagavatā taṃ attanau buddhānubhāvaina dhammaṃ ñāpaituṃ na sak‍kāti? āma na sak‍kā, na hi parataughausamantaraina sāvakānaṃ dhammābhisamayau sambhavati, añ‍ñathā itarapac‍cayarahitassapi dhammābhisamayaina bhavitabbaṃ, na ca taṃ atthi| vuttañhaitaṃ – ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, pac‍cayā sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāya paratau ca ghausau ajjhattañ‍ca yaunisaumanasikārau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 2.127)|

udakauti tassa nāmaṃ, rāmassa pana puttatāya rāmaputtau| abhidausakālakatauti aḍḍharattai kālakatau| bhagavataupi khau ñāṇaṃ udapādīti idhāpi kira bhagavā daivatāya kathitavacanaina san‍niṭṭhānaṃ akatvā sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaina aulaukaintau ‘‘hiyyau aḍḍharattai kālaṃ katvā udakau rāmaputtau  naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai nibbattau’’ti addasa, tasmā aivaṃ vuttaṃ| saisaṃ purimasadisamaiva|

bahūpakārāti bahuupakārā| padhānapahitattaṃ upaṭṭhahiṃsūti padhānatthāya paisitattabhāvaṃ vasanaṭṭhānai parivaiṇasammaj‍janaina pattacīvaraṃ gahaitvā anubandhanaina mukhaudakadantakaṭṭhadānādinā ca upaṭṭhahiṃsu| kai panaitai pañ‍cavaggiyā nāma? yai tai –

rāmau dhajau lakkhaṇau cāpi mantī|

kauṇḍañ‍ñau ca bhaujau suyāmau sudattau|

aitai tadā aṭṭha ahaisuṃ brāhmaṇā|

chaḷaṅgavā mantaṃ viyākariṃsūti|| (ma॰ ni॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.284; jā॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.nidānakathā; apa॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.avidūrainidānakathā)|

baudhisattassa jātakālai supinapaṭiggāhakā caiva lakkhaṇapaṭiggāhakā ca aṭṭha brāhmaṇā| taisu tayau dvaidhā byākariṃsu ‘‘imaihi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau agāraṃ ajjhāvasamānau rājā hauhiti cak‍kavattī, pabbajamānau buddhau’’ti| pañ‍ca brāhmaṇā aikaṃsabyākaraṇā ahaisuṃ  ‘‘imaihi lakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau agārai na tiṭṭhati, buddhauva hautī’’ti| taisu purimā tayau yathāmantapadaṃ gatā| aitai hi lakkhaṇamantasaṅkhātavaidavacanānurūpaṃ paṭipan‍nā dvai gatiyau bhavanti anañ‍ñāti vuttaniyāmaina nicchinituṃ asak‍kauntā vuttamaiva paṭipaj‍jiṃsu, na mahāpurisassa buddhabhāvappattiṃ pac‍cāsīsiṃsu| imai pana kauṇḍañ‍ñādayau pañ‍ca ‘‘aikaṃsatau buddhau bhavissatī’’ti jātanicchayattā mantapadaṃ atik‍kantā| tai attanā laddhaṃ tuṭṭhidānaṃ ñātakānaṃ vissaj‍jaitvā ‘‘ayaṃ mahāpurisau agārai na ajjhāvasissati, aikantaina buddhau bhavissatī’’ti nibbaimatikā baudhisattaṃ uddissa samaṇapabbaj‍jaṃ pabbajitā, taisaṃ puttātipi vadanti, taṃ aṭṭhakathāyaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ| aitai kira daharakālaiva bahū mantai jāniṃsu, tasmā nai brāhmaṇā ācariyaṭṭhānai ṭhapayiṃsu| tai ‘‘pacchā amhaihi puttadārajaṭaṃ chinditvā na sak‍kā bhavissati pabbajitu’’nti daharakālaiyaiva pabbajitvā ramaṇīyāni saināsanāni paribhuñ‍jantā vicariṃsu| kālaina kālaṃ pana ‘‘kiṃ bhau mahāpurisau mahābhinikkhamanaṃ nikkhantau’’ti pucchanti| manussā ‘‘kuhiṃ tumhai mahāpurisaṃ passissatha, tīsu pāsādaisu vividhanāṭakamajjhai daivau viya sampattiṃ anubhautī’’ti vadanti| tai sutvā ‘‘na tāva mahāpurisassa ñāṇaṃ paripākaṃ gacchatī’’ti appaussuk‍kā vihariṃsuyaiva|

kasmā  panaittha bhagavā ‘‘bahukārā khau mai pañ‍cavaggiyā’’ti āha| kiṃ upakārakānaṃyaiva aisa dhammaṃ daisaiti, anupakārakānaṃ na daisaitīti? nau na daisaiti| paricayavasaina haisa āḷārañ‍caiva kālāmaṃ udakañ‍ca rāmaputtaṃ aulaukaisi| aitasmiṃ pana buddhakkhaittai ṭhapaitvā añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñaṃ añ‍ñau paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ sacchikātuṃ samatthau nāma natthi| kasmā? tathāvidhaupanissayattā| pubbai kira puñ‍ñakaraṇakālai dvai bhātarau ahaisuṃ| tai ca aikatau sassaṃ akaṃsu| tattha jaiṭṭhassa ‘‘aikasmiṃ sassai nava vārai aggasassadānaṃ mayā dātabba’’nti ahausi| sau vappakālai bījaggaṃ nāma datvā gabbhakālai kaniṭṭhaina saddhiṃ mantaisi ‘‘gabbhakālai gabbhaṃ phālaitvā dassāmī’’ti| kaniṭṭhau ‘‘taruṇasassaṃ nāsaitukāmausī’’ti āha| jaiṭṭhau kaniṭṭhassa ananuvattanabhāvaṃ ñatvā khaittaṃ vibhajitvā attanau kauṭṭhāsatau gabbhaṃ phālaitvā khīraṃ nīharitvā sappiphāṇitaina yaujaitvā adāsi, puthukakālai puthukaṃ kāraitvā adāsi, lāyanai lāyanaggaṃ, vaiṇikaraṇai vaiṇaggaṃ, vaiṇiyau purisabhāravasaina bandhitvā kalāpakaraṇai kalāpaggaṃ, khalai kalāpānaṃ ṭhapanadivasai khalaggaṃ, madditvā vīhīnaṃ rāsikaraṇadivasai khalabhaṇḍaggaṃ, kauṭṭhāgārai dhañ‍ñassa pakkhipanadivasai kauṭṭhagganti aivaṃ aikasmiṃ sassai nava vārai aggadānaṃ adāsi| kaniṭṭhau pana khalatau dhañ‍ñaṃ uddharitvā gahaṇadivasai adāsi| taisu jaiṭṭhau añ‍ñāsikauṇḍañ‍ñatthairau jātau, kaniṭṭhau subhaddaparibbājakau| iti aikasmiṃ sassai navan‍naṃ aggadānānaṃ din‍nattā ṭhapaitvā thairaṃ añ‍ñau paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ sacchikātuṃ samatthau nāma natthi| ‘‘navan‍naṃ aggadānānaṃ din‍nattā’’ti idañ‍ca tassa rattañ‍ñūnaṃ aggabhāvatthāya katābhinīhārānurūpaṃ pavattitasāvakapāramiyā ciṇṇantai pavattitattā vuttaṃ| tiṇṇampi hi baudhisattānaṃ taṃtaṃpāramiyā sikhāppattakālai pavattitaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ apuñ‍ñaṃ vā garutaravipākamaiva hauti, dhammassa ca sabbapaṭhamaṃ sacchikiriyāya vinā kathaṃ rattañ‍ñūnaṃ aggabhāvasiddhīti? ‘‘bahukārā khau mai pañ‍cavaggiyā’’ti idaṃ pana upakārānussaraṇamattakainaiva vuttaṃ|

isipatanai migadāyaiti tasmiṃ kira padaisai anuppan‍nai buddhai pac‍caikasambuddhā gandhamādanapabbatai sattāhaṃ niraudhasamāpattiyā vītināmaitvā niraudhā vuṭṭhāya nāgalatādantakaṭṭhaṃ khāditvā anautattadahai mukhaṃ dhauvitvā pattacīvaramādāya ākāsaina āgantvā nipatanti| tattha cīvaraṃ pārupitvā nagarai piṇḍāya caritvā katabhattakic‍cā gamanakālaipi tatauyaiva uppatitvā gacchanti| iti isayau aittha nipatanti uppatanti cāti taṃ ṭhānaṃ ‘‘isipatana’’nti saṅkhaṃ gataṃ, migānaṃ pana abhayatthāya din‍nattā ‘‘migadāyau’’ti vuc‍cati| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘isipatanai migadāyai’’ti| añ‍ñai buddhā paṭhamaṃ dhammadaisanatthāya gacchantā ākāsaina gantvā tatthaiva autaranti, amhākaṃ pana bhagavā upakassa ājīvakassa upanissayaṃ disvā ‘‘upakau imaṃ addhānaṃ  paṭipan‍nau, sau maṃ disvā sal‍lapitvā gamissati, atha puna nibbin‍nau āgamma arahattaṃ sacchikarissatī’’ti ñatvā aṭṭhārasayaujanaṃ maggaṃ padasāva agamāsi| taina vuttaṃ ‘‘yaina bārāṇasī, taina cārikaṃ pak‍kāmī’’ti|

11. antarā ca gayaṃ antarā ca baudhinti gayāya ca baudhissa ca vivarai tigāvutantarai ṭhānai| baudhimaṇḍatau hi gayā tīṇi gāvutāni, bārāṇasī  aṭṭhārasa yaujanāni| upakau baudhimaṇḍassa ca gayāya ca antarai bhagavantaṃ addasa| antarā-saddaina pana yuttattā upayaugavacanaṃ kataṃ| īdisaisu ca ṭhānaisu akkharacintakā ‘‘antarā gāmañ‍ca nadiñ‍ca yātī’’ti aivaṃ aikamaiva antarā-saddaṃ payuj‍janti, sau dutiyapadainapi yaujaitabbau hauti, ayaujiyamānai upayaugavacanaṃ na pāpuṇāti sāmivacanassa pasaṅgai antarā-saddayaugaina upayaugavacanassa icchitattā| idha pana yaujaitvā aiva vuttau| addhānamagganti addhānasaṅkhātaṃ maggaṃ, dīghamagganti atthau| addhānagamanasamayassa vibhaṅgai ‘‘addhayaujanaṃ gacchissāmīti bhuñ‍jitabba’’ntiādivacanatau (pāci॰ 218) addhayaujanampi addhānamaggau hauti| baudhimaṇḍatau pana gayā tigāvutaṃ| vippasan‍nānīti suṭṭhu pasan‍nāni| indriyānīti manacchaṭṭhāni indriyāni| parisuddhauti niddausau| pariyaudātauti tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| nirupak‍kilaisatāyaiva hi aisa ‘‘pariyaudātau’’ti vuttau, na saitabhāvaina| aitassa pariyaudātataṃ disvāva indriyānaṃ vippasan‍nataṃ añ‍ñāsi, nayaggāhīpañ‍ñā kiraisā tassa ājīvakassa|

sabbābhibhūti sabbaṃ taibhūmakadhammaṃ abhibhavitvā ṭhitau| sabbavidūti sabbaṃ catubhūmakadhammaṃ avaidiṃ añ‍ñāsiṃ sabbasau ñaiyyāvaraṇassa pahīnattā| sabbaisu dhammaisu anūpalittauti sabbaisu taibhūmakadhammaisu raj‍janadussanamuyhanādinā kilaisalaipaina alittau| sabbañ‍jahauti sabbaṃ taibhūmakadhammaṃ jahitvā ṭhitau| appahātabbampi hi kusalābyākataṃ tappaṭibaddhakilaisappahānaina pahīnattā na hautīti jahitamaiva hauti| taṇhakkhayai vimuttauti taṇhakkhayai nibbānai ārammaṇakaraṇavasaina vimuttau| sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāyāti sabbaṃ catubhūmakadhammaṃ attanāva jānitvā| kamuddisaiyyanti kaṃ añ‍ñaṃ ‘‘ayaṃ mai ācariyau’’ti uddisaiyyaṃ|

na mai ācariyau atthīti laukuttaradhammai mayhaṃ ācariyau nāma natthi| kiñ‍cāpi hi laukiyadhammānampi yādisau laukanāthassa adhigamau, na tādisau adhigamau parūpadaisau atthi, laukuttaradhammai panassa laisaupi natthi| natthi mai paṭipuggalauti mayhaṃ sīlādīhi guṇaihi paṭinidhibhūtau puggalau nāma natthi| sammāsambuddhauti haitunā nayaina cattāri sac‍cāni sayaṃ buddhau| sītibhūtauti sabbakilaisagginibbāpanaina sītibhūtau, kilaisānaṃ yaiva nibbutattā nibbutau|

kāsinaṃ  puranti kāsiraṭṭhai nagaraṃ| āhañchanti āhanissāmi| amatadundubhinti vainaiyyānaṃ amatādhigamāya ugghausanādiṃ katvā satthu dhammadaisanā ‘‘amatadundubhī’’ti vuttā, dhammacak‍kapaṭilābhāya taṃ amatabhairiṃ paharissāmīti gacchāmīti vuttaṃ hauti|

arahasi anantajinauti anantajinaupi bhavituṃ yuttauti atthau| anantañāṇau jitakilaisauti anantajinau| hupaiyyapāvusauti āvusau aivampi nāma bhavaiyya, aivaṃvidhai nāma rūparatanai īdisaina ñāṇaina bhavitabbanti adhippāyau| ayañhissa pabbaj‍jāya pac‍cayau jātau| katādhikārau haisa| tathā hi bhagavā taina samāgamanatthaṃ padasāva taṃ maggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji| pak‍kāmīti vaṅkahārajanapadaṃ nāma agamāsi|

tatthaikaṃ migaluddakagāmakaṃ nissāya vāsaṃ kappaisi, jaiṭṭhakaluddakau taṃ upaṭṭhāsi| tasmiñ‍ca janapadai caṇḍā makkhikā haunti| atha naṃ aikāya cāṭiyā vasāpaisuṃ| migaluddakau dūraṃ migavaṃ gacchantau ‘‘amhākaṃ arahantai mā pamaj‍jī’’ti cāpaṃ nāma dhītaraṃ āṇāpaitvā agamāsi saddhiṃ puttabhātukaihi| sā cassa dhītā dassanīyā hauti kauṭṭhāsasampan‍nā| dutiyadivasai upakau gharaṃ āgatau taṃ dārikaṃ sabbaṃ upacāraṃ katvā parivisituṃ upagataṃ disvā rāgaina abhibhūtau bhuñ‍jitumpi asak‍kauntau bhājanaina bhattaṃ ādāya vasanaṭṭhānaṃ gantvā bhattaṃ aikamantaṃ nikkhipitvā ‘‘sacai cāpaṃ labhāmi, jīvāmi| nau cai, marāmī’’ti nirāhārau sayi| sattamai divasai māgavikau āgantvā dhītaraṃ upakassa pavattiṃ pucchi| sā ‘‘aikadivasamaiva āgantvā puna nāgatapubbau’’ti āha|

māgavikau āgatavaisainaiva naṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchissāmīti taṅkhaṇaṃyaiva gantvā ‘‘kiṃ, bhantai, aphāsuka’’nti pādai parāmasantau pucchi| upakau nitthunantau parivattatiyaiva| sau ‘‘vada bhantai, yaṃ mayā sak‍kā kātuṃ, sabbaṃ karissāmī’’ti āha| upakau ‘‘sacai cāpaṃ labhāmi, jīvāmi, nau cai, mayhamaiva maraṇaṃ saiyyau’’ti āha| jānāsi kira, bhantai, kiñ‍ci sippanti? na jānāmīti| na, bhantai, kiñ‍ci sippaṃ ajānantaina sak‍kā gharāvāsaṃ adhiṭṭhātunti| sau āha ‘‘nāhaṃ kiñ‍ci sippaṃ jānāmi, apica  tumhākaṃ maṃsahārakau bhavissāmi, maṃsañ‍ca vik‍kiṇissāmī’’ti| māgavikau ‘‘amhākampi aitadaiva ruc‍catī’’ti uttarasāṭakaṃ  datvā gharaṃ ānaitvā dhītaraṃ adāsi| taisaṃ saṃvāsamanvāya puttau vijāyi, ‘‘subhaddau’’tissa nāmaṃ akaṃsu| cāpā tassa raudanakālai ‘‘maṃsahārakassa putta migaluddakassa putta mā raudi mā raudī’’tiādīni vadamānā puttatausanagītaina upakaṃ uppaṇḍaisi| ‘‘bhaddai tvaṃ maṃ anāthauti mañ‍ñasi, atthi mai anantajinau nāma sahāyau, tassāhaṃ santikaṃ gamissāmī’’ti āha| cāpā ‘‘aivamayaṃ aṭṭīyatī’’ti ñatvā punappunaṃ kathaisi| sau aikadivasaṃ anāraucaitvāva majjhimadaisābhimukhau pak‍kāmi|

bhagavā ca taina samayaina sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai, atha khau bhagavā paṭikac‍caiva bhikkhū āṇāpaisi ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, anantajinauti pucchamānau āgacchati, tassa maṃ dassaiyyāthā’’ti| upakaupi khau ‘‘kuhiṃ anantajinau vasatī’’ti pucchantau anupubbaina sāvatthiṃ āgantvā vihāramajjhai ṭhatvā ‘‘kuhiṃ anantajinau’’ti pucchi| taṃ bhikkhū bhagavatau santikaṃ nayiṃsu| sau ca bhagavantaṃ disvā ‘‘sañ‍jānātha maṃ bhagavā’’ti āha| āma upaka sañ‍jānāmi, kuhiṃ pana tvaṃ vasitthāti| vaṅkahārajanapadai, bhantaiti| upaka mahal‍lakausi jātau, pabbajituṃ sakkhissasīti| pabbajissāmi, bhantaiti| bhagavā pabbājaitvā tassa kammaṭṭhānaṃ adāsi| sau kammaṭṭhānai kammaṃ karauntau anāgāmiphalai patiṭṭhāya kālaṃ katvā avihaisu nibbattau, nibbattikkhaṇaiyaiva ca arahattaṃ pāpuṇi| avihai nibbattamattā hi satta janā arahattaṃ pāpuṇiṃsu, taisaṃ sau añ‍ñatarau| vuttañhaitaṃ –

‘‘avihaṃ upapan‍nāsai, vimuttā satta bhikkhavau|

rāgadausaparikkhīṇā, tiṇṇā laukai visattikaṃ||

‘‘upakau palagaṇḍau ca, puk‍kusāti ca tai tayau|

bhaddiyau khaṇḍadaivau ca, bāhuraggi ca saṅgiyau|

tai hitvā mānusaṃ daihaṃ, dibbayaugaṃ upac‍cagu’’nti|| (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.50, 105)|

12. saṇṭhapaisunti ‘‘naiva abhivādaitabbau’’tiādinā katikaṃ akaṃsu| bāhul‍likauti cīvarabāhul‍lādīnaṃ atthāya paṭipan‍nau| padhānavibbhantauti  padhānatau pubbai anuṭṭhitaduk‍karacaraṇatau vibbhantau bhaṭṭhau parihīnau| āvattau bāhul‍lāyāti cīvarādibahubhāvatthāya āvattau| apica khau āsanaṃ ṭhapaitabbanti apica khau panassa uc‍cakulai nibbattassa āsanamattaṃ  ṭhapaitabbanti vadiṃsu| asaṇṭhahantāti buddhānubhāvaina buddhataijaina abhibhūtā attanau katikāya ṭhātuṃ asak‍kauntā| nāmaina ca āvusauvādaina ca samudācarantīti ‘‘gautamā’’ti ca ‘‘āvusau’’ti ca vadanti, ‘‘āvusau gautama, mayaṃ uruvailāyaṃ padhānakālai tuyhaṃ pattacīvaraṃ gahaitvā vicarimha, mukhaudakaṃ dantakaṭṭhaṃ adamha, vutthaparivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jimha, pacchā tai kau vattapaṭipattiṃ akāsi, kac‍ci amhaisu pak‍kantaisu na cintayitthā’’ti aivarūpaṃ kathaṃ kathaintīti atthau|

na cirassaivāti acirainaiva| kulaputtāti duvidhā kulaputtā jātikulaputtā ācārakulaputtā ca, aitai pana ubhayathāpi kulaputtāyaiva| agārasmāti gharā| agārāya hitaṃ agāriyaṃ, kasigaurakkhādi kuṭumbapausanakammaṃ vuc‍cati| natthi aittha agāriyanti anagāriyaṃ| pabbaj‍jāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| pabbajantīti upagacchanti upasaṅkamanti| tadanuttaranti taṃ anuttaraṃ| brahmacariyapariyausānanti maggabrahmacariyassa pariyausānaṃ, arahattaphalanti vuttaṃ hauti| tassa hi atthāya kulaputtā pabbajanti| diṭṭhaiva dhammaiti tasmiṃyaiva attabhāvai| sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvāti attanauyaiva pañ‍ñāya pac‍cakkhaṃ katvā, aparappac‍cayaṃ katvāti atthau| upasampaj‍ja viharissathāti pāpuṇitvā sampādaitvā viharissatha|

iriyāyāti duk‍karairiyāya| paṭipadāyāti duk‍karapaṭipattiyā| duk‍karakārikāyāti pasatapasatamuggayūsādiāharaṇādinā duk‍karakaraṇaina| uttari manussadhammāti manussadhammatau upari| alaṃ ariyaṃ kātunti alamariyau, ariyabhāvāya samatthauti vuttaṃ hauti, ñāṇadassanamaiva ñāṇadassanavisaisau, alamariyau ca sau ñāṇadassanavisaisau cāti alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau| ñāṇadassananti ca dibbacakkhupi vipassanāpi maggaupi phalampi pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇampi sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇampi vuc‍cati| ‘‘appamattau samānau ñāṇadassanaṃ ārādhaitī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.311) hi aittha dibbacakkhu ñāṇadassanaṃ nāma| ‘‘ñāṇadassanāya cittaṃ abhinīharati abhinin‍nāmaitī’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.235) aittha  vipassanāñāṇaṃ| ‘‘abhabbā tai ñāṇadassanāya anuttarāya sambaudhāyā’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 4.196) aittha maggau| ‘‘ayamañ‍ñau uttarimanussadhammā alamariyañāṇadassanavisaisau adhigatau phāsuvihārau’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.328) aittha phalaṃ| ‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi ‘akuppā mai caitauvimutti, ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1081; mahāva॰ 16) aittha pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇaṃ| ‘‘ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi ‘sattāhakālakatau āḷārau kālāmau’’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.284; 2.340; mahāva॰ 10)  aittha sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇaṃ| idha pana sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇapadaṭṭhānau ariyamaggau sabbañ‍ñutañ‍ñāṇamaiva vā adhippaitaṃ|

abhijānātha mai nauti abhijānātha nu mai| aivarūpaṃ pabhāvitamaitanti aittha aivarūpaṃ vākyabhaidanti atthau, api nu ahaṃ uruvailāyaṃ padhānai tumhākaṃ saṅgaṇhanatthaṃ anuk‍kaṇṭhanatthaṃ rattiṃ vā divā vā āgantvā ‘‘āvusau, mayaṃ yattha katthaci gamissāmāti mā vitak‍kayittha, mayhaṃ aubhāsau vā kammaṭṭhānanimittaṃ vā pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti aivarūpaṃ kañ‍ci vacanabhaidaṃ akāsinti adhippāyau| tai aikapadainaiva satiṃ labhitvā uppan‍nagāravā ‘‘addhā aisa buddhau jātau’’ti saddahitvā ‘‘nau haitaṃ bhantai’’ti āhaṃsu| asakkhi khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū sañ‍ñāpaitunti bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū ‘‘buddhau aha’’nti jānāpaituṃ asakkhi| añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaisunti añ‍ñāya arahattappattiyā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaisuṃ abhinīhariṃsu|

dhammacak‍kappavattanasuttavaṇṇanā

13. dvaimai, bhikkhavai, antāti dvai imai, bhikkhavai, kauṭṭhāsā, dvai bhāgāti atthau| bhāgavacanau haittha anta-saddau ‘‘pubbantai ñāṇaṃ aparantai ñāṇa’’ntiādīsu (dha॰ sa॰ 1063) viya| imassa pana padassa uc‍cāraṇasamakālaṃ pavattanigghausau buddhānubhāvaina haiṭṭhā avīciṃ upari bhavaggaṃ patvā dasasahassilaukadhātuṃ pharitvā aṭṭhāsi| tasmiṃyaiva samayai paripak‍kakusalamūlā sac‍cābhisambaudhāya katādhikārā aṭṭhārasakauṭisaṅkhā brahmānau samāgacchiṃsu| pacchimadisāya sūriyau atthamaiti, pācīnadisāya āsāḷhanakkhattaina yuttau puṇṇacandau uggacchati| tasmiṃ samayai bhagavā  dhammacak‍kappavattanasuttaṃ ārabhantau ‘‘dvaimai, bhikkhavai, antā’’tiādimāha|

tattha pabbajitaināti gihibandhanaṃ chaitvā pabbaj‍jupagataina| na saivitabbāti na vaḷañ‍jaitabbā nānuyuñ‍jitabbā| yau cāyaṃ kāmaisu kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugauti yau ca ayaṃ vatthukāmaisu kilaisakāmasukhassa anuyaugau, kilaisakāmasaṃyuttassa sukhassa anugatauti atthau| hīnauti lāmakau| gammauti gāmavāsīnaṃ santakau taihi saivitabbatāya| pauthuj‍janikauti puthuj‍janaina andhabālajanaina āciṇṇau| anariyauti na ariyau na visuddhau na uttamau, na vā ariyānaṃ santakau| anatthasaṃhitauti na atthasaṃhitau, hitasukhāvahakāraṇaṃ anissitauti atthau| attakilamathānuyaugauti attanau kilamathassa anuyaugau, dukkhakaraṇaṃ dukkhuppādananti atthau| dukkhauti  kaṇṭakāpassayasaiyyādīhi attabādhanaihi dukkhāvahau| majjhimā paṭipadāti ariyamaggaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ| maggau hi kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugau aikau antau, attakilamathānuyaugau aikau antau, aitai dvai antai na upaiti na upagacchati, vimuttau aitaihi antaihi, tasmā ‘‘majjhimā paṭipadā’’ti vuc‍cati| aitaisaṃ majjhai bhavattā majjhimā, vaṭṭadukkhanissaraṇatthikaihi paṭipaj‍jitabbatau ca paṭipadāti, tathā laubhau aikau antau, dausau aikau antau| sassataṃ aikaṃ antaṃ, ucchaidau aikau antauti purimanayainaiva vitthāraitabbaṃ|

cakkhukaraṇītiādīhi tamaiva paṭipadaṃ thaumaiti| pañ‍ñācakkhuṃ karautīti cakkhukaraṇī| sā hi catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ dassanāya saṃvattati pariñ‍ñābhisamayādibhaidassa dassanassa pavattanaṭṭhaināti ‘‘cakkhukaraṇī’’ti vuc‍cati| tayidaṃ satipi paṭipadāya anañ‍ñattai avayavavasaina sijjhamānau atthau samudāyaina katau nāma hautīti upacāravasaina vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ| dutiyapadaṃ tassaiva vaivacanaṃ| upasamāyāti kilaisupasamatthāya| abhiñ‍ñāyāti catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ abhijānanatthāya| sambaudhāyāti taisaṃyaiva sambujjhanatthāya| nibbānāyāti nibbānasacchikiriyāya| atha vā nibbānāyāti anupādisaisanibbānāya| ‘‘upasamāyā’’ti hi iminā saupādisaisanibbānaṃ gahitaṃ|

idāni  taṃ majjhimappaṭipadaṃ sarūpatau dassaitukāmau ‘‘katamā ca sā’’ti pucchitvā ‘‘ayamaivā’’tiādinā nayaina vissaj‍jaisi| tattha ayamaivāti avadhāraṇavacanaṃ añ‍ñassa nibbānagāmimaggassa atthibhāvapaṭisaidhanatthaṃ| sattāpaṭikkhaipau hi idha paṭisaidhanaṃ alabbhamānattā añ‍ñassa maggassa| ariyauti kilaisānaṃ ārakattā ariyau niruttinayaina| aripahānāya saṃvattatītipi ariyau arayau pāpadhammā yanti apagacchanti aitaināti katvā| ariyaina bhagavatā daisitattā ariyassa ayantipi ariyau, ariyabhāvappaṭilābhāya saṃvattatītipi ariyau| aittha pana ariyakarau ariyautipi uttarapadalaupaina ariya-saddasiddhi vaiditabbā| aṭṭhahi aṅgaihi upaitattā aṭṭhaṅgikau| maggaṅgasamudāyai hi maggavauhārau, samudāyau ca samudāyīhi saman‍nāgatau nāma hauti| ayaṃ panaittha vacanatthau – attanau avayavabhūtāni aṭṭhaṅgāni aitassa santīti aṭṭhaṅgikauti| paramatthatau pana aṅgāniyaiva maggau pañ‍caṅgikatūriyādīni viya, na ca aṅgavinimuttau chaḷaṅgau vaidau viya| kilaisai māraintau gacchatīti maggau niruttinayaina, nibbānaṃ maggati gavaisatīti vā maggau| ariyamaggau hi nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karauntau gavaisantau viya hauti| nibbānatthikaihi maggīyatīti vā maggau vivaṭṭūpanissayapuñ‍ñakaraṇatau  paṭṭhāya tadatthapaṭipattitau| gammati vā taihi paṭipaj‍jīyatīti maggau| aittha pana ādiantavipariyāyaina saddasiddhi vaiditabbā|

saiyyathidanti nipātau, tassa katamau sau iti caiti atthau, katamāni vā tāni aṭṭhaṅgānīti| sabbaliṅgavibhattivacanasādhāraṇau hi ayaṃ nipātau| aikamaikampi aṅgaṃ maggauyaiva| yathāha ‘‘sammādiṭṭhi maggau caiva haitu cā’’ti (dha॰ sa॰ 1039)| paurāṇāpi bhaṇanti ‘‘dassanamaggau sammādiṭṭhi, abhiniraupanamaggau sammāsaṅkappau…pai॰… avikkhaipamaggau sammāsamādhī’’ti| nanu ca aṅgāni samuditāni maggau antamasau sattaṅgavikalassa ariyamaggassa abhāvatauti? sac‍camaitaṃ sac‍casampaṭivaidhai, maggappac‍cayatāya pana yathāsakaṃ kic‍cakaraṇaina pac‍caikampi tāni maggauyaiva, añ‍ñathā samuditānampi taisaṃ maggakic‍caṃ na sambhavaiyyāti| sammādiṭṭhiādīsu sammā passatīti sammādiṭṭhi, sammā saṅkappaiti sampayuttadhammai  nibbānasaṅkhātai ārammaṇai abhiniraupaitīti sammāsaṅkappau, sammā vadati aitāyāti sammāvācā, sammā karauti aitaināti sammākammaṃ, tadaiva sammākammantau, sammā ājīvati aitaināti sammāājīvau, sammā vāyamati ussahatīti sammāvāyāmau, sammā sarati anussaratīti sammāsati, sammā samādhiyati cittaṃ aitaināti sammāsamādhīti aivaṃ nibbacanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ| idāni ayaṃ khau sā bhikkhavaiti tamaiva paṭipadaṃ nigamaintau āha| tassatthau – yvāyaṃ cattāraupi laukuttaramaggai aikatau katvā kathitau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, ayaṃ khau sā bhikkhavai…pai॰… nibbānāya saṃvattatīti|

14. aivaṃ majjhimapaṭipadaṃ sarūpatau dassaitvā idāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni dassaituṃ ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai’’tiādimāha| tattha (visuddhi॰ 2.530) dukkhanti aittha du-iti ayaṃ saddau kucchitai dissati| kucchitañhi puttaṃ ‘‘duputtau’’ti vadanti, khaṃ-saddau pana tucchai| tucchañhi ākāsaṃ ‘‘kha’’nti vuc‍cati| idañ‍ca paṭhamasac‍caṃ kucchitaṃ anaikaupaddavādhiṭṭhānatau, tucchaṃ bālajanaparikappitadhuvasubhasukhattabhāvavirahitatau, tasmā kucchitattā tucchattā ca ‘‘dukkha’’nti vuc‍cati| yasmā panaitaṃ buddhādayau ariyā paṭivijjhanti, tasmā ‘‘ariyasac‍ca’’nti vuc‍cati| ariyapaṭivijjhitabbañhi sac‍caṃ purimapadai uttarapadalaupaina ‘‘ariyasac‍ca’’nti vuttaṃ| ariyassa tathāgatassa sac‍cantipi ariyasac‍caṃ| tathāgataina hi sayaṃ adhigatattā pavaiditattā tatau aiva ca añ‍ñaihi adhigamanīyattā taṃ tassa hautīti| atha vā aitassa abhisambuddhattā ariyabhāvasiddhitau ariyasādhakaṃ sac‍cantipi ariyasac‍caṃ pubbai viya uttarapadalaupaina| avitathabhāvaina vā araṇīyattā adhigantabbattā ariyaṃ sac‍cantipi ariyasac‍caṃ| sac‍catthaṃ pana catun‍nampi sac‍cānaṃ paratau aikajjhaṃ dassayissāma|

idāni taṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ sarūpatau dassaituṃ ‘‘jātipi dukkhā’’tiādimāha| tatrāyaṃ jāti-saddau anaikatthau| tathā haisa ‘‘aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.31; ma॰ ni॰ 1.148) aittha bhavai āgatau| ‘‘atthi, visākhai, nigaṇṭhā nāma samaṇajātī’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 3.71) aittha nikāyai| ‘‘jāti dvīhi khandhaihi saṅgahitā’’ti (dhātu॰ 71) aittha saṅkhatalakkhaṇai| ‘‘yaṃ mātukucchismiṃ paṭhamaṃ cittaṃ  uppan‍naṃ paṭhamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pātubhūtaṃ, tadupādāya sāvassa jātī’’ti (mahāva॰ 124) aittha paṭisandhiyaṃ| ‘‘sampatijātau, ānanda, baudhisattau’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 2.31; ma॰ ni॰ 3.207) aittha pasūtiyaṃ| ‘‘akkhittau anupakuṭṭhau jātivādainā’’ti (dī॰ ni॰ 1.303) aittha kulai| svāyamidha gabbhasaiyyakānaṃ paṭisandhitau paṭṭhāya yāva mātukucchimhā nikkhamanaṃ, tāva pavattaisu khandhaisu, itaraisaṃ paṭisandhikkhaṇaisvaivāti daṭṭhabbau| ayampi ca pariyāyakathāva, nippariyāyatau pana tattha tattha nibbattamānānaṃ sattānaṃ yai khandhā pātubhavanti, taisaṃ paṭhamapātubhāvau jāti nāma|

kasmā panaisā dukkhāti cai? anaikaisaṃ dukkhānaṃ vatthubhāvatau| anaikāni hi dukkhāni| saiyyathidaṃ – dukkhadukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadukkhaṃ saṅkhāradukkhaṃ paṭicchan‍nadukkhaṃ appaṭicchan‍nadukkhaṃ pariyāyadukkhaṃ nippariyāyadukkhanti| tattha kāyikacaitasikā dukkhā vaidanā sabhāvatau ca nāmatau ca dukkhattā dukkhadukkhanti vuc‍cati| sukhā vaidanā vipariṇāmadukkhuppattihaitutau vipariṇāmadukkhaṃ| upaikkhā vaidanā caiva saisā ca taibhūmakā saṅkhārā udayabbayapīḷitattā saṅkhāradukkhaṃ| kaṇṇasūladantasūlarāgajapariḷāhadausajapariḷāhādikāyikacaitasikā ābādhā pucchitvā jānitabbatau upak‍kamassa ca apākaṭabhāvatau paṭicchan‍nadukkhaṃ| dvattiṃsakammakāraṇādisamuṭṭhānau ābādhau apucchitvāva jānitabbatau upak‍kamassa ca pākaṭabhāvatau appaṭicchan‍nadukkhaṃ| ṭhapaitvā dukkhadukkhaṃ saisadukkhaṃ sac‍cavibhaṅgai āgataṃ jātiādi sabbampi tassa tassa dukkhassa vatthubhāvatau pariyāyadukkhaṃ| dukkhadukkhaṃ pana nippariyāyadukkhanti vuc‍cati| tatrāyaṃ jāti yaṃ taṃ bālapaṇḍitasuttādīsu (ma॰ ni॰ 3.246 ādayau) bhagavatāpi upamāvasaina pakāsitaṃ āpāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yañ‍ca sugatiyampi manussalaukai gabbhauk‍kantimūlakādibhaidaṃ dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jati, tassa vatthubhāvatau dukkhā| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘jāyaitha  nau cai narakaisu sattau|

tatthaggidāhādikamappasayhaṃ|

labhaitha dukkhaṃ na kuhiṃ patiṭṭhaṃ|

ic‍cāha dukkhāti munīdha jātiṃ||

‘‘dukkhaṃ  tiracchaisu kasāpatauda-

daṇḍābhighātādibhavaṃ anaikaṃ|

yaṃ taṃ kathaṃ tattha bhavaiyya jātiṃ|

vinā tahiṃ jāti tataupi dukkhā||

‘‘paitaisu dukkhaṃ pana khuppipāsā-

vātātapādippabhavaṃ vicittaṃ|

yasmā ajātassa na tattha atthi|

tasmāpi dukkhaṃ muni jātimāha||

‘‘tibbandhakārai ca asayha sītai|

laukantarai yaṃ asuraisu dukkhaṃ|

na taṃ bhavai tattha na cassa jāti|

yatau ayaṃ jāti tataupi dukkhā||

‘‘yañ‍cāpi gūthanarakai viya mātu gabbhai|

sattau vasaṃ ciramatau bahi nikkhamañ‍ca|

pappauti dukkhamatighauramidampi natthi|

jātiṃ vinā itipi jāti ayañhi dukkhā||

‘‘kiṃ bhāsitaina bahunā nanu yaṃ kuhiñ‍ci|

atthīdha kiñ‍cidapi dukkhamidaṃ kadāci|

naivatthi jātivirahaina yatau mahaisi|

dukkhāti sabbapaṭhamaṃ imamāha jāti’’nti|| (visuddhi॰ 2.541; vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 191; mahāni॰ aṭṭha॰ 5; paṭi॰ ma॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.32-33)|

jarāpi  dukkhāti aittha pana duvidhā jarā saṅkhatalakkhaṇañ‍ca khaṇḍic‍cādisammatau santatiyaṃ aikabhavapariyāpan‍nakkhandhapurāṇabhāvau ca, sā idha adhippaitā| sā panaisā jarā saṅkhāradukkhabhāvatau caiva dukkhavatthutau ca dukkhā| yañhi aṅgapac‍caṅgasithilabhāvaindriyavikāravirūpatāyaubbanavināsavīriyāvisādasatimativippavāsaparaparibhavādianaikapac‍cayaṃ kāyikacaitasikaṃ dukkhamuppaj‍jati, jarā tassa vatthu| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘aṅgānaṃ sithilībhāvā, indriyānaṃ vikāratau|

yaubbanassa vināsaina, balassa upaghātatau||

‘‘vippavāsā  satādīnaṃ, puttadāraihi attanau|

appasādanīyatau caiva, bhiyyau bālattapattiyā||

‘‘pappauti dukkhaṃ yaṃ mac‍cau, kāyikaṃ mānasaṃ tathā|

sabbamaitaṃ jarāhaitu, yasmā tasmā jarā dukhā’’ti|| (visuddhi॰ 2.542; vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 192; mahāni॰ aṭṭha॰ 5; paṭi॰ ma॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.32-33)|

byādhipi dukkhauti idaṃ padaṃ vibhaṅgai dukkhasac‍caniddaisapāḷiyaṃ na āgataṃ, tainaiva visuddhimaggaipi dukkhasac‍caniddaisai taṃ na uddhaṭaṃ, dhammacak‍kapavattanasuttantapāḷiyaṃyaiva pana upalabbhati, tasmā tatthaivimassa vacanai añ‍ñattha ca avacanai kāraṇaṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ|

maraṇampi dukkhanti aitthāpi duvidhaṃ maraṇaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇañ‍ca| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘jarāmaraṇaṃ dvīhi khandhaihi saṅgahita’’nti (dhātu॰ 71)| aikabhavapariyāpan‍najīvitindriyappabandhavicchaidau ca| yaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ ‘‘nic‍caṃ maraṇatau bhaya’’nti (su॰ ni॰ 581; jā॰ 1.11.88), taṃ idha adhippaitaṃ| jātipac‍cayamaraṇaṃ upak‍kamamaraṇaṃ sarasamaraṇaṃ āyukkhayamaraṇaṃ puñ‍ñakkhayamaraṇantipi tassaiva nāmaṃ| tayidaṃ dukkhassa vatthubhāvatau dukkhanti vaiditabbaṃ| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘pāpassa  pāpakammādi-nimittamanupassatau|

bhaddassāpasahantassa, viyaugaṃ piyavatthukaṃ|

mīyamānassa yaṃ dukkhaṃ, mānasaṃ avisaisatau||

‘‘sabbaisañ‍cāpi yaṃ sandhi-bandhanacchaidanādikaṃ|

vituj‍jamānamammānaṃ, hauti dukkhaṃ sarīrajaṃ||

‘‘asayhamappaṭikāraṃ, dukkhassaitassidaṃ yatau|

maraṇaṃ vatthu tainaitaṃ, dukkhamic‍caiva bhāsita’’nti|| (visuddhi॰ 2.543; vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 193; mahāni॰ aṭṭha॰ 5; paṭi॰ ma॰ aṭṭha॰ 1.1.32-33)|

imasmiñ‍ca ṭhānai ‘‘saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsāpi dukkhā’’ti vibhaṅgai dukkhasac‍caniddaisai āgataṃ, idha pana taṃ natthi, tatthāpi kāraṇaṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ|

appiyaihi sampayaugau dukkhauti aittha appiyasampayaugau nāma amanāpaihi sattasaṅkhāraihi samaudhānaṃ| saupi dukkhavatthutau dukkhau| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘disvāva  appiyai dukkhaṃ, paṭhamaṃ hauti caitasi|

tadupak‍kamasambhūta-matha kāyai yatau idha||

‘‘tatau dukkhadvayassāpi, vatthutau sau mahaisinā|

dukkhau vuttauti viñ‍ñaiyyau, appiyaihi samāgamau’’ti||

piyaihi vippayaugau dukkhauti aittha pana piyavippayaugau nāma manāpaihi sattasaṅkhāraihi vinābhāvau| saupi saukadukkhassa vatthutau dukkhau| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘ñātidhanādiviyaugā|

saukasarasamappitā vituj‍janti|

bālā  yatau tatauyaṃ|

dukkhauti matau piyavippayaugau’’ti||

yampicchaṃ na labhatīti aittha ‘‘ahau vata mayaṃ na jātidhammā assāmā’’tiādīsu alabbhanaiyyavatthūsu icchāva ‘‘yampicchaṃ na labhati, tampi dukkha’’nti vuttā, sāpi dukkhavatthutau dukkhā| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘taṃ taṃ patthayamānānaṃ, tassa tassa alābhatau|

yaṃ vighātamayaṃ dukkhaṃ, sattānaṃ idha jāyati||

‘‘alabbhanaiyyavatthūnaṃ, patthanā tassa kāraṇaṃ|

yasmā tasmā jinau dukkhaṃ, icchitālābhamabravī’’ti||

saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhāti aittha pana yasmā indhanamiva pāvakau, lakkhamiva paharaṇāni, gaurūpaṃ viya ḍaṃsamakasādayau, khaittamiva lāyakā, gāmaṃ viya gāmaghātakā, upādānakkhandhapañ‍cakamaiva jātiādayau nānappakāraihi vibādhaintā tiṇalatādīni viya bhūmiyaṃ, pupphaphalapal‍lavāni viya rukkhaisu upādānakkhandhaisuyaiva nibbattanti, upādānakkhandhānañ‍ca ādidukkhaṃ jāti, majjhaidukkhaṃ jarā, pariyausānadukkhaṃ maraṇaṃ, manaurathavighātappattānañ‍ca icchāvighātadukkhaṃ icchitālābhauti aivaṃ nānappakāratau upaparikkhiyamānā upādānakkhandhāva dukkhāti yadaitaṃ aikamaikaṃ dassaitvā vuc‍camānaṃ anaikaihipi kappaihi na sak‍kā anavasaisatau vattuṃ, tasmā taṃ sabbampi dukkhaṃ aikajalabindumhi sakalasamuddajalarasaṃ viya yaisu kaisuci pañ‍cupādānakkhandhaisu saṅkhipitvā dassaituṃ ‘‘saṃkhittaina  pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā’’ti bhagavā avauca| taināhu paurāṇā –

‘‘jātippabhutikaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ vuttamidha tādinā|

avuttaṃ yañ‍ca taṃ sabbaṃ, vinā aitai na vij‍jati||

‘‘yasmā tasmā upādāna-kkhandhā saṅkhaipatau imai|

dukkhāti vuttā dukkhanta-daisakaina mahaisinā’’ti||

aivaṃ  sarūpatau dukkhasac‍caṃ dassaitvā idāni samudayasac‍caṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudaya’’ntiādimāha| tattha saṃ-iti ayaṃ saddau ‘‘samāgamau samaita’’ntiādīsu saṃyaugaṃ dīpaiti, u-iti ayaṃ ‘‘uppan‍naṃ udita’’ntiādīsu uppattiṃ| aya-saddau pana kāraṇaṃ dīpaiti| idañ‍cāpi dutiyasac‍caṃ avasaisapac‍cayasamāyaugai sati dukkhassuppattikāraṇanti dukkhassa saṃyaugai uppattikāraṇattā ‘‘dukkhasamudaya’’nti vuc‍cati| yāyaṃ taṇhāti yā ayaṃ taṇhā| paunaubbhavikāti punabbhavakaraṇaṃ punabbhavau uttarapadalaupaina, punabbhavau sīlamaitissāti paunaubbhavikā| nandīrāgaina sahagatāti nandīrāgasahagatā| idaṃ vuttaṃ hauti ‘‘nandanatau raj‍janatau ca nandīrāgabhāvaṃ sabbāsu avatthāsu appac‍cakkhāya vuttiyā nandīrāgasahagatā’’ti| tatratatrābhinandinīti yatra yatra attabhāvau nibbattati, tatratatrābhinandinī|

saiyyathidanti nipātau, tassa sā katamāti caiti ayamatthau| rūpataṇhādibhaidaina chabbidhāyaiva taṇhā pavattiākārabhaidatau kāmataṇhādivasaina tividhā vuttā| rūpataṇhāyaiva hi yadā cakkhussa āpāthamāgataṃ rūpārammaṇaṃ kāmassādavasaina assādayamānā pavattati, tadā kāmataṇhā nāma hauti| yadā tadaivārammaṇaṃ dhuvaṃ sassatanti pavattāya sassatadiṭṭhiyā saddhiṃ pavattati, tadā bhavataṇhā nāma hauti| sassatadiṭṭhisahagatau hi rāgau ‘‘bhavataṇhā’’ti vuc‍cati| yadā pana tadaivārammaṇaṃ ucchij‍jati vinassatīti pavattāya ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā saddhiṃ pavattati, tadā vibhavataṇhā nāma hauti| ucchaidadiṭṭhisahagatau hi rāgau ‘‘vibhavataṇhā’’ti vuc‍cati| aisa nayau saddataṇhādīsupi|

kasmā  panaittha taṇhāva samudayasac‍caṃ vuttāti? visaisahaitubhāvatau| avij‍jā hi bhavaisu ādīnavaṃ paṭicchādaintī diṭṭhiādiupādānañ‍ca tattha tattha abhinivisamānaṃ taṇhaṃ abhivaḍḍhaiti, dausādayaupi kammassa kāraṇaṃ haunti, taṇhā pana taṃtaṃbhavayaunigativiñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitisattāāvāsasattanikāyakulabhaugissariyādivicittataṃ abhipatthaintī kammavicittatāya upanissayataṃ kammassa ca sahāyabhāvaṃ upagacchantī bhavādivicittataṃ niyamaiti, tasmā dukkhassa visaisahaitubhāvatau añ‍ñaisupi avij‍jāupādānakammādīsu suttai abhidhammai ca avasaisakilaisākusalamūlādīsu vuttaisu dukkhahaitūsu vij‍jamānaisu taṇhāva ‘‘samudayasac‍ca’’nti vuttāti vaiditabbaṃ|

idāni dukkhaniraudhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudha’’ntiādimāha| tattha yasmā ni-saddau abhāvaṃ, raudha-saddau ca cārakaṃ dīpaiti, tasmā abhāvau aittha saṃsāracārakasaṅkhātassa dukkharaudhassa sabbagatisuñ‍ñattā, samadhigatai vā tasmiṃ saṃsāracārakasaṅkhātassa  dukkharaudhassa abhāvau hauti tappaṭipakkhattātipi ‘‘dukkhaniraudha’’nti vuc‍cati| dukkhassa vā anuppādaniraudhapac‍cayattā dukkhaniraudhaṃ| dukkhaniraudhaṃ dassaintaina caittha ‘‘yau tassāyaiva taṇhāyā’’tiādinā nayaina samudayaniraudhau vuttau, sau kasmā vuttauti cai? samudayaniraudhaina dukkhaniraudhau| byādhinimittavūpasamaina byādhivūpasamau viya hi haituniraudhaina phalaniraudhau, tasmā samudayaniraudhainaiva dukkhaṃ nirujjhati, na añ‍ñathā| taināha –

‘‘yathāpi mūlai anupaddavai daḷhai|

chin‍naupi rukkhau punaraiva rūhati|

aivampi taṇhānusayai anūhatai|

nibbattatī dukkhamidaṃ punappuna’’nti|| (dha॰ pa॰ 338)|

iti yasmā samudayaniraudhainaiva dukkhaṃ nirujjhati, tasmā bhagavā dukkhaniraudhaṃ dassaintau samudayaniraudhaina daisaisi| sīhasamānavuttinau hi tathāgatā| tai dukkhaṃ niraudhaintā dukkhaniraudhañ‍ca daisaintā haitumhi paṭipaj‍janti, na phalai| yathā hi sīhau yainattani sarau khittau, tatthaiva attanau  balaṃ dassaiti, na sarai, tathā buddhānaṃ kāraṇai paṭipatti, na phalai| titthiyā pana suvānavuttinau| tai dukkhaṃ niraudhaintā dukkhaniraudhañ‍ca daisaintā attakilamathānuyaugadaisanādīhi phalai paṭipaj‍janti, na haitumhi| yathā hi sunakhā kainaci laiḍḍuppahārai din‍nai bhussantā laiḍḍuṃ khādanti, na pahāradāyakai uṭṭhahanti, aivaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyā dukkhaṃ niraudhaitukāmā kāyakhaidamanuyuj‍janti, na kilaisaniraudhanaṃ, aivaṃ tāva dukkhaniraudhassa samudayaniraudhavasaina daisanāya payaujanaṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

ayaṃ panaittha atthau| tassāyaiva taṇhāyāti tassā ‘‘paunaubbhavikā’’ti vatvā kāmataṇhādivasaina vibhattataṇhāya| virāgau vuc‍cati maggau| ‘‘virāgā vimuc‍catī’’ti (ma॰ ni॰ 1.245; saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.12, 59) hi vuttaṃ| virāgaina niraudhau virāganiraudhau, anusayasamugghātatau asaisau virāganiraudhau asaisavirāganiraudhau| atha vā virāgauti pahānaṃ vuc‍cati, tasmā anusayasamugghātatau asaisau virāgau asaisau niraudhauti aivampaittha yaujanā daṭṭhabbā, atthatau pana sabbānaiva aitāni nibbānassa vaivacanāni| paramatthatau hi dukkhaniraudhau ariyasac‍canti nibbānaṃ vuc‍cati| yasmā pana taṃ āgamma taṇhā viraj‍jati caiva nirujjhati ca, tasmā ‘‘virāgau’’ti ca ‘‘niraudhau’’ti ca vuc‍cati| yasmā ca tadaiva āgamma tassā cāgādayau  haunti, kāmaguṇālayādīsu caittha aikaupi ālayau natthi, tasmā cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayauti vuc‍cati|

idāni dukkhaniraudhagāminipaṭipadāariyasac‍caṃ dassaituṃ ‘‘idaṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī’’tiādimāha| yasmā panaitaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ dukkhaniraudhaṃ gacchati ārammaṇavasaaina tadabhimukhabhūtattā, paṭipadā ca hauti dukkhaniraudhappattiyā, tasmā ‘‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti vuc‍cati| saisamaittha vuttanayamaiva| kau pana naisaṃ dukkhādīnaṃ sac‍caṭṭhauti? yau pañ‍ñācakkhunā upaparikkhiyamānānaṃ māyāva viparītau, marīciva visaṃvādakau, titthiyānaṃ attā viya anupalabbhasabhāvau ca na hauti, atha khau bādhanappabhavasantiniyyānappakāraina tacchāviparītabhūtabhāvaina ariyañāṇassa gaucarau hautiyaiva, aisa aggilakkhaṇaṃ viya laukapakati viya ca tacchāviparītabhūtabhāvau sac‍caṭṭhauti vaiditabbau| aittha ca aggilakkhaṇaṃ nāma uṇhattaṃ| tañhi katthaci kaṭṭhādiupādānabhaidai visaṃvādakaṃ viparītaṃ  abhūtaṃ vā kadācipi na hauti, ‘‘jātidhammā jarādhammā, athau maraṇadhamminau’’ti (a॰ ni॰ 5.57) aivaṃ vuttajātiādikā laukapakatīti vaiditabbā| ‘‘aikac‍cānaṃ tiracchānānaṃ tiriyaṃ dīghatā, manussādīnaṃ uddhaṃ dīghatā, vuddhiniṭṭhappattānaṃ puna avaḍḍhananti aivamādikā ca laukapakatī’’ti vadanti|

apica –

nābādhakaṃ yatau dukkhaṃ, dukkhā añ‍ñaṃ na bādhakaṃ|

bādhakattaniyāmaina, tatau sac‍camidaṃ mataṃ||

taṃ vinā nāñ‍ñatau dukkhaṃ, na hauti na ca taṃ tatau|

dukkhahaituniyāmaina, iti sac‍caṃ visattikā||

nāñ‍ñā nibbānatau santi, santaṃ na ca na taṃ yatau|

santabhāvaniyāmaina, tatau sac‍camidaṃ mataṃ||

maggā añ‍ñaṃ na niyyānaṃ, aniyyānau na cāpi sau|

tacchaniyyānabhāvattā, iti sau sac‍casammatau||

iti  tacchāvipal‍lāsa-bhūtabhāvaṃ catūsupi|

dukkhādīsvavisaisaina, sac‍caṭṭhaṃ āhu paṇḍitāti|| (vibha॰ aṭṭha॰ 189)|

15. pubbai ananussutaisūti itau pubbai ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’ntiādinā na anussutaisu assutapubbaisu catusac‍cadhammaisu| cakkhuntiādīni ñāṇavaivacanānaiva| ñāṇamaiva haittha pac‍cakkhatau dassanaṭṭhaina cakkhu viyāti cakkhu, ñāṇaṭṭhaina ñāṇaṃ, pakāratau jānanaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñā, paṭivijjhanaṭṭhaina vij‍jā, sac‍cappaṭicchādakassa mauhandhakārassa vidhamanatau aubhāsanaṭṭhaina ālaukauti vuttaṃ| taṃ panaitaṃ catūsu sac‍caisu laukiyalaukuttaramissakaṃ niddiṭṭhanti vaiditabbaṃ|

16. yāvakīvañ‍cāti yattakaṃ kālaṃ| tiparivaṭṭanti sac‍cañāṇakic‍cañāṇakatañāṇasaṅkhātānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ parivaṭṭānaṃ vasaina tiparivaṭṭaṃ| sac‍cañāṇādivasaina hi tayau parivaṭṭā aitassāti tiparivaṭṭanti vuc‍cati ñāṇadassanaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, idaṃ dukkhasamudaya’’nti aivaṃ catūsu sac‍caisu yathābhūtañāṇaṃ sac‍cañāṇaṃ nāma| taisuyaiva ‘‘pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ  pahātabbaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ bhāvaitabba’’nti aivaṃ kattabbakic‍cajānanañāṇaṃ kic‍cañāṇaṃ nāma| ‘‘pariñ‍ñātaṃ pahīnaṃ sacchikataṃ bhāvita’’nti aivaṃ tassa katabhāvajānanañāṇaṃ katañāṇaṃ nāma| dvādasākāranti taisaṃyaiva aikaikasmiṃ sac‍cai tiṇṇaṃ tiṇṇaṃ ākārānaṃ vasaina dvādasākāraṃ| ñāṇadassananti aitaisaṃ tiparivaṭṭānaṃ dvādasan‍naṃ ākārānaṃ vasaina uppan‍nañāṇasaṅkhātaṃ dassanaṃ|

anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhinti uttaravirahitaṃ sabbasaiṭṭhaṃ sammā sāmañ‍ca baudhiṃ| atha vā pasatthaṃ sundarañ‍ca baudhiṃ| baudhīti ca bhagavatau arahattamaggau idhādhippaitau| sāvakānaṃ arahattamaggau anuttarā baudhi hauti, na hautīti? na hauti| kasmā? asabbaguṇadāyakattā| taisañhi kassaci arahattamaggau arahattaphalamaiva daiti, kassaci tissau vij‍jā, kassaci cha abhiñ‍ñā, kassaci catassau paṭisambhidā, kassaci sāvakapāramīñāṇaṃ| pac‍caikabuddhānampi pac‍caikabaudhiñāṇamaiva daiti, buddhānaṃ pana sabbaguṇasampattiṃ daiti abhisaikau viya rañ‍ñau sabbalaukissariyabhāvaṃ| tasmā añ‍ñassa kassacipi anuttarā baudhi na hautīti| abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsinti abhisambuddhau ahaṃ pattau paṭivijjhitvā ṭhitauti aivaṃ paṭijāniṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādīti adhigataguṇānaṃ yāthāvatau dassanasamatthaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇañāṇañ‍ca pana mai udapādi| akuppā mai vimuttīti ayaṃ mayhaṃ arahattaphalavimutti akuppā paṭipakkhaihi na kaupaitabbāti aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ udapādi| tattha dvīhākāraihi akuppatā vaiditabbā maggasaṅkhātakāraṇatau ca ārammaṇatau ca| sā hi catūhi maggaihi samucchin‍nakilaisānaṃ  puna anivattanatāya kāraṇataupi akuppā, akuppadhammaṃ nibbānaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā pavattatāya ārammaṇataupi akuppā anākuppārammaṇānaṃ laukiyasamāpattīnaṃ tadabhāvatau| antimāti pacchimā| natthi dāni punabbhavauti idāni puna añ‍ñau bhavau nāma natthīti|

imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasminti imasmiṃ niggāthakai suttai| niggāthakañhi suttaṃ pucchāvissaj‍janasahitaṃ ‘‘vaiyyākaraṇa’’nti vuc‍cati| bhañ‍ñamānaiti bhaṇiyamānai, daisiyamānaiti atthau| virajanti apāyagamanīyarāgarajādīnaṃ vigamaina virajaṃ| vītamalanti anavasaisadiṭṭhivicikicchāmalāpagamaina vītamalaṃ| paṭhamamaggavajjhakilaisarajābhāvaina vā virajaṃ, pañ‍cavidhadussīlyamalāpagamaina vītamalaṃ| dhammacakkhunti brahmāyusuttai (ma॰ ni॰ 2.383 ādayau) haiṭṭhimā tayau maggā vuttā, cūḷarāhulauvādai (ma॰ ni॰ 3.416 ādayau) āsavakkhayau, idha pana sautāpattimaggau adhippaitau| catusac‍casaṅkhātaisu dhammaisu taisaṃ dassanaṭṭhaina cakkhūti dhammacakkhu, haiṭṭhimaisu vā tīsu maggadhammaisu aikaṃ sautāpattimaggasaṅkhātaṃ cakkhūti dhammacakkhu, samathavipassanādhammanibbattatāya sīlāditividhadhammakkhandhabhūtatāya vā dhammamayaṃ cakkhūtipi dhammacakkhu, tassa uppattiākāradassanatthaṃ ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci samudayadhammaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ niraudhadhamma’’nti āha| nanu ca maggañāṇaṃ asaṅkhatadhammārammaṇaṃ, na saṅkhatadhammārammaṇanti? sac‍camaitaṃ, yasmā taṃ niraudhaṃ ārammaṇaṃ katvā kic‍cavasaina sabbasaṅkhataṃ asammauhappaṭivaidhavasaina paṭivijjhantaṃ uppaj‍jati, tasmā tathā vuttaṃ|
